# P3 · M4 API and screens

!!! info "Running since 2026-10-30 · milestone M4 of step P3 ([P2 §4](../../plans/P2-execution.md#4-m4-api-and-screens)) · board gap G1 done (Paper chrome on the lite shared components), merged as #85 · board gap G3a done (Paper page bodies of the run boards), merged as #88 · board gap G2 done (the launch boards), merged as #89 · Task 17 done (inspect a pipeline and launch a run, with G5's API tests), merged as #95 · Task 18 done (read the runs, tail a log, cancel, and the live stream), merged as #97 · Task 19 done (the screens served beside the API, the shell and the launch form, with G5's UI tests), merged as #99 · board gap G4 done (the Paper shell in `web/`, light and dark, at three widths), merged as #101 · Task 20 done (Home, the runs list, the run detail with Cancel, and the Playwright smoke), merged as #103 · UI parity A done (the shell, Home, the runs list and the run detail against the lite boards), merged as #105 · UI parity B done (the phone, the Launch form and the Head word after a run ends), merged as #107 · UI parity C done (parameters one per line, an icon on every Launch section, the nf-core mark, the run's Copy menu, and opening and reading the logs), merged as #109 · UI parity D done on `autopilot/P3-parD` (stage, process and task details, a clickability inventory, log search, test pipelines in Launch, parameter schemas for the synthetic pipelines), its `review-code`: approve after 2 rounds · public demo (P3-demo) done on `autopilot/P3-demo` (the 0.0.1 screens at `/demo/` of the docs site, answered from a recording, read-only), its `review-code`: approve after 3 rounds; its PR changes `previews.yml` and waits for Thomas's review · Task 21 to do"

![P3 UI parity D: run detail sheets, searchable logs, a test pipeline in Launch. web/src and the synthetic pipelines' schemas, what Thomas asked on 2026-10-31, two review rounds. Section 1, the run page, where every stage, process and task opens its detail (Thomas: steps and stages not clickable, no details). Panel 1, click a row, a chip or a link: an rnaseq run with the chips 14 succeeded, 1 running, 1 failed, 1 cached, 2 retried; the pipeline directory with Copy; Why it failed with First to fail: ALIGN (S09) and a link Process ALIGN; stage 1 QC 12 of 12; stage 2 Align open with Stage details and a process row ALIGN, running, 3 of 5, under the pointer. Panel 2, the sheet for ALIGN, NFCORE:RNASEQ:ALIGN, running, Stage 2 Align, chips 3 of 5 done, 1 running, 0 pending, 1 failed, 1 cached, 2 retried, Open in Tasks, its tasks ALIGN (S01) succeeded and ALIGN (S02) running, Load more tasks, 50 at a time; a right sheet of 560 px, full screen on a phone, aria-modal, Escape or Close, focus back on the row that opened it. Panel 3, a task: ALIGN (S02), Back to ALIGN, ID tsk_2, Name, Tag S02, State running, Process ALIGN as a link, Stage 2 Align, and a note: not in Spectio 0.0.1 yet, its files, its work directory, exit code, duration and resources; they arrive with P1 Tasks 28 and 29. Each view stacks and Back pops it. Beside it: Open in Tasks closes the sheet and opens the Tasks tab on that process, every state, ?tab=tasks&process=; a count chip opens the Tasks tab on its filter, Succeeded, Running, Pending or Failed; cached and retried stay plain. Pins: R1 CR-P3PARD-06 fixed in 2a45be98, a double click on Load more fetched the same page twice, now one page in flight with aria-busy; R2 CR-P3PARD-07 open, the native disabled moves keyboard focus to the body; R1 CR-P3PARD-01 fixed, a chip on the Tasks view already shown left focus pending, focusTasks now focuses the panel at once; R1 CR-P3PARD-05 fixed, First to fail looked in the first 50 failed tasks only, now it follows page.next up to 250, drawn as bars of 50 and 250. Section 2, the logs (Thomas: logs not searchable): the Logs tab at ?tab=logs&source=engine with a search field holding ALIGN, the counter 1 / 4, previous and next buttons, and the level buttons All 5, INFO 3, WARN 1, ERROR 1; five log lines from the step's test with ALIGN marked four times, case-insensitive, the current match brighter. The keys: Enter the next match, Shift+Enter the previous one, Escape clears; the query lands 150 ms after the last key; the counter is a polite live region, or No match; a level button shows only its lines and the search follows it. A log that ends with a newline, before: All 3 and INFO 3 with an empty last line; after: All 2 and INFO 2 (R1 CR-P3PARD-04, fixed in 2a45be98, here and in the Summary's tail). Open: R1 CR-P3PARD-02, every line gets the query's length so every line re-renders on each query; R1 CR-P3PARD-03, the Summary's 6-line tail has no search. Section 3, Launch: try a pipeline this server ran (Thomas: no default test workflow in Launch). Home's Your pipelines lists three pipeline directories, each a link to /launch?location=, filled and loaded. Launch shows Try a test pipeline with two buttons, the pipeline field filled with a synthetic Nextflow path, Load, and the Loaded card, 4 parameter groups, profiles local, slurm, test. A path that starts with / is filled and loaded at once; a repository is filled and the Revision field takes the focus. Try a test pipeline lists the distinct pipelines of the last 50 runs, newest first, at most five; with none, a link to the docs on the synthetic test pipelines. The schemas nextflow_schema.json and config.schema.yaml have the same four groups, each engine with its own keys, Nextflow then Snakemake: Input/output options, input and outdir, then samples and outdir; Scenario, chunks, scale and seed for both; Failure injection, fail_mode, fail_process, fail_sample and fail_gb, then fail_mode, fail_rule, fail_sample and fail_gb; Resources, time_limit_s and python_image, then time_limit_s only. Open pins: R1 SEC-P3PARD-01, a crafted /launch?location= link loads a planted pipeline under a name it chooses, Launch still needs a click; R1 SEC-P3PARD-02, no upper bound on chunks, scale, fail_gb or time_limit_s; R1 SEC-P3PARD-03, python_image takes any string, a mutable tag can replace the digest pin. Section 4, two review rounds, one cell per finding: R1 code-reviewer 8 minors with ticks on 01, 04, 05 and 06, security 3 minors, approve and approve; R2 code-reviewer 1 minor, 07, security none, approve and approve. IDs restart each round. Fixes 2a45be98: CR-P3PARD-01, 04, 05, 06; simplifier 846f4621, LogViewer Line has one render path, 9 lines out, 1 in; pytest 511 passed, 15 deselected; tsc clean; vitest 175 of 175. Counts, paths and IDs are illustrative; the log lines and the sheet's counts come from the step's tests.](../../assets/diagrams/p3-pard-sheets-search_light.png#only-light)

![P3-demo: the real screens as a public demo, answered from a recording. npm run build:demo, vite --mode demo, at spectio.pages.dev/demo/ and on every PR preview; reads only, nothing leaves the page. Section 1, record a demo server: a demo spectio serve on node login1, account alice, HOME /g/demo-lab, on 127.0.0.1; python3 tools/media/record_demo.py --base http://127.0.0.1:8766 records every GET, each list whole, per run its processes, stages, tasks and 3 logs, and 3 pipeline inspections; the System: Linux release is cut to System: Linux in the engine logs; nothing is written if sanitize.leaks, the host name or the kernel release hits. SEC-P3DEMO-02 (round 1) fixed in 06b1117; SEC-P3DEMO-07 (round 2) open, the release is still in the history of f769837. Section 2, one public file: web/demo/fixtures.json, committed, demo data only, 41 answers, 773 205 bytes, drawn to scale against the 1.5 MB budget: tasks 371 690, logs 328 643, the rest 11 909; 5 runs recorded, 4 succeeded and 1 failed in MERGE; cohort-2026-scale has 2069 tasks, 333 029 bytes. CR-P3DEMO-07 and SEC-P3DEMO-03 (round 3) open: no CI job runs test_record_demo.py. Section 3, two builds kept apart: build:demo writes web/dist-demo with relative asset paths and routes in the hash, copied by previews.yml to site/demo and checked with curl; the normal build writes web/dist, and check-dist.mjs fails on any of the 5 run IDs or the 3 demo texts. CR-P3DEMO-01 (round 1) open: if the docs get a demo page, cp -R lands in site/demo/dist-demo. Section 4, the real screens read-only: the banner on every page, and Launch disabled with the read-only reason; Open raw is a blob of the text shown. Section 5, the stub answers every request: a recorded GET gets its answer, lists filtered and paged as the server does; any write 403 read-only-demo; not recorded or another origin 404, an unrecorded pipeline 422 naming the three; the event stream opens and stays quiet; every JSON time moved by one offset so the newest run ended an hour before now, logs keep theirs. Open minors pinned: SEC-P3DEMO-04 no CSP on /demo/, CR-P3DEMO-03 the 422 beyond the brief, CR-P3DEMO-04 the read-only reason comes last. Section 6, the banner and the sticky sidebar in a 1280 by 800 window to scale: at f769837 the sidebar foot sat about 31 px under the fold (CR-P3DEMO-02, fixed); at 06b1117 the banner was one 31 px line cut by an ellipsis and its disclosure hidden on the phone (CR-P3DEMO-03, major, round 2, fixed); at 25aa0c9 and 8244642 the banner is 49 px with its whole text and the sidebar ends at 790 of 800, measured into --sp-demo-banner-h by a ResizeObserver; on a 375 px phone the whole text wraps. Bottom: three review rounds, one cell per finding: round 1, 9 findings, both reviewers approve; round 2, 6 findings, code-reviewer changes with one major fixed, security approve; round 3, 6 findings, both approve. No blocker; the simplifier put the sidebar offset in one rule, shell.css, 4 lines out and 1 in; pytest 511 passed, vitest 158 passed, tsc clean, before and after.](../../assets/diagrams/p3-demo-static-demo_light.png#only-light)

![P3 UI parity C: Launch rows and icons, the logos, the run's Copy menu and logs. web/src and the two Launch boards, what Thomas asked on 2026-10-31 against the Lite boards, three review rounds. Section 1, Launch: one parameter per line, an icon on every section. Before: Parameters in a grid of 3 columns, the help and the meta in one grey line, plain section titles with no icon. After: one parameter per row with the input full width; a line icon on every section (Pipeline, Parameters, Where it runs, and the preview on its dark card); the Pipeline card above the grid; Launch in the page head too; new group icons filter, tag, shield, building, signpost, download. One row drawn large: outdir with string · directory-path on the right, the input, the help under it; skip_trimming with boolean · changed · default false; a screen reader hears one text, help · meta. Pins: R1 CR-P3PARC-01, major, a boolean row had no meta, fixed in ae60a735; R2 CR-P3PARC-04, minor, open, an error shows after the help. Section 2, the logos, one rule, markFor, inbox 91 answer (b), je veux les logos, important: nf-core/rnaseq on nextflow shows the nf-core mark; lab/chipseq on nextflow the Nextflow mark; lab/variants on snakemake the Snakemake mark; an unknown engine none, Launch shows a folder icon. Where it shows and at what size: the runs list 16 to 20 px, Home 18 to 22 px, the run header tile 26 px, the Pipeline fact 18 px, the Launch Loaded card 24 px; the Engine fact and the Launch engine buttons keep the engine's mark at 16 px. Pin: R1 and R2 SEC-P3PARC-02, minor, open, the name is a claim any fork can make; the logos' upstream source stays to be validated. Section 3, the run page: the header with Cancel run and a Copy menu of Config (params.json), Run directory and Run ID, a status Copied the run ID for 4 s; pin R2 CR-P3PARC-03, minor, open, the board says Parameters, recorded in ui-spec §4.4, the board follows at the next /apply-findings; pin R1 CR-P3PARC-04, minor, open, the status line is display none while empty. The Logs tab at ?tab=logs&source=head-err: Head output, Head errors, Engine log; Reload, Download last 64 KiB, Open raw; a File row with the path under launch/.spectio/head.err and Copy; a dark viewer opened at its end, no wrap, with level tints for ERROR, WARN and retry, completed and Finished job, and progress N; a note linking to open a task in Tasks. Open raw opens the same text as a page in a new tab, text/plain, nosniff, no-store; pin R1 and R2 SEC-P3PARC-01, minor, open, no CSP and no Referrer-Policy. Pin R1 CR-P3PARC-05, minor, open, any line with completed is green. The URL keeps the tab and the source: Logs, pick Head errors, Summary, Logs again returns to Head errors through lastSource; CR-P3PARC-02, fixed in 4506ab5f with a test written first. Section 4, three review rounds, one cell per finding: R1 code-reviewer 1 major, 5 minors, 1 simplify, ticks on 01, 02, 06 and 07, security 2 minors, changes and approve; R2 code-reviewer 3 minors and 1 simplify, a tick on 02, security 2 minors, approve and approve; R3 no finding, approve and approve. The simplifier, 089adc38, the Pipeline section's indentation, whitespace only, 101 lines out, 101 in; pytest 511 passed, tsc clean, vitest 140 of 140 in 15 files; eslint not run, the web package has no config.](../../assets/diagrams/p3-parc-launch-logs_light.png#only-light)

![P3 UI parity B: the phone, the Launch form and the Head word, against the Lite boards. web/src and one runner revert; fixes UX-PAR-08 to 10 and 30 to 34 of ui-parity-0.0.1; four review rounds. Section 1, the phone before and after, drawn to scale on a 390 px screen: before, the runs list was five squeezed columns with 36 px targets and no title; after, Runs is stacked run cards with pills that scroll sideways (10); Home has no title row, its greeting stays in the page, with count cards two by two and its lists as cards (10); Launch has 44 px fold heads and a full-width Launch (09); the run has Back to Runs, 44 px, and its title is the h1 (08). Two close-ups: the run's title row, a 44 px back link named Back to Runs, the page title as the h1 with its sub line; Launch's pinned bar, the summary nf-core/rnaseq 3.27.0 · Nextflow · singularity · 2 values changed, or the reason it cannot launch, above a 48 px Launch, linked by aria-describedby. Section 2, the Launch form: Nextflow groups that fold, each with a line icon from its fa_icon and a status (2 required · filled, 2 changed, defaults), open when a required field is empty or a value changed (30); a Snakemake config whose map ref is a section of dotted keys ref.species and ref.release (31); one value set sends the whole ref map, the others at their defaults (CR-P3PARB-04 r2); on Nextflow a map stays one JSON field (R3 CR-P3PARB-02). One function, buildParams, writes both the POST /runs body and the preview in the aside, YAML for Snakemake, params.json for Nextflow (32). Ticks: R1 SEC-P3PARB-01, major, a schema key __proto__ wrote into Object.prototype, fixed in 8c86467f; R4 CR-P3PARB-R4-01, major, a typed JSON null sent as unset, fixed in dcec010e. Section 3, the Head word after a run ends: on a time line the run ends, the runner keeps reading the spool for its 60 s end grace, then reports the head's end and exited_at; the run page says Alive, then Ending during the grace, then Exited. The rejected change 2f6809b5, reverted in 7993752a, set exited_at at once, so the server dropped the launch and stopped reading the spool, and events after onFlowComplete could be lost (R3 CR-P3PARB-01, major, fixed). Section 4, the four review rounds, one cell per finding: R1 approve and changes, R2 approve and approve, R3 changes and approve, R4 changes and approve with no security finding; the red cells are SEC-P3PARB-01, CR-P3PARB-R4-01 and CR-P3PARB-01, all fixed on the branch; R4 was made by hand after two fix rounds without approval](../../assets/diagrams/p3-parb-phone-launch_light.png#only-light)

Section 1, the phone before and after, drawn to scale on a 390 px screen: before, the runs list was five squeezed columns with 36 px targets and no title; after, Runs is stacked run cards with pills that scroll sideways (10); Home has no title row, its greeting stays in the page, with count cards two by two and its lists as cards (10); Launch has 44 px fold heads and a full-width Launch (09); the run has Back to Runs, 44 px, and its title is the h1 (08). Two close-ups: the run's title row, a 44 px back link named Back to Runs, the page title as the h1 with its sub line; Launch's pinned bar, the summary nf-core/rnaseq 3.27.0 · Nextflow · singularity · 2 values changed, or the reason it cannot launch, above a 48 px Launch, linked by aria-describedby. Section 2, the Launch form: Nextflow groups that fold, each with a line icon from its fa_icon and a status (2 required · filled, 2 changed, defaults), open when a required field is empty or a value changed (30); a Snakemake config whose map ref is a section of dotted keys ref.species and ref.release (31); one value set sends the whole ref map, the others at their defaults (CR-P3PARB-04 r2); on Nextflow a map stays one JSON field (R3 CR-P3PARB-02). One function, buildParams, writes both the POST /runs body and the preview in the aside, YAML for Snakemake, params.json for Nextflow (32). Ticks: R1 SEC-P3PARB-01, major, a schema key __proto__ wrote into Object.prototype, fixed in 8c86467f; R4 CR-P3PARB-R4-01, major, a typed JSON null sent as unset, fixed in dcec010e. Section 3, the Head word after a run ends: on a time line the run ends, the runner keeps reading the spool for its 60 s end grace, then reports the head's end and exited_at; the run page says Alive, then Ending during the grace, then Exited. The rejected change 2f6809b5, reverted in 7993752a, set exited_at at once, so the server dropped the launch and stopped reading the spool, and events after onFlowComplete could be lost (R3 CR-P3PARB-01, major, fixed). Section 4, the four review rounds, one cell per finding: R1 approve and changes, R2 approve and approve, R3 changes and approve, R4 changes and approve with no security finding; the red cells are SEC-P3PARB-01, CR-P3PARB-R4-01 and CR-P3PARB-01, all fixed on the branch; R4 was made by hand after two fix rounds without approval](../../assets/diagrams/p3-parb-phone-launch_dark.png#only-dark)

![P3 UI parity A: the shell, Home, the runs list and the run, against the Lite boards. web/src only, within the 0.0.1 API; fixes UX-PAR-01 to 07, 11 to 27 and 29 of ui-parity-0.0.1; the engine mark only, no nf-core mark yet, inbox 91. Top row, four sketched screens. 1, the shell: Runs stays lit on a run's page (06), a badge counts the active runs (07), page heads with a 48 px tile (03), sections fold, kept per screen (02). 2, Home at /: count cards by state, each a link to /runs; a rail and the engine mark on each row; a failed run says why, never blank (16); Where your runs go, from GET /version. 3, the runs list at /runs?state=failed&q=sar: the state filter and the name in the URL, the name landing 250 ms after typing, each segment with its count, Snakemake runs counted in jobs (17). 4, the run at /runs/id: one header on every tab with facts and task chips; Why it failed opens first on a failed run; stages and processes with bars (24); an aside with log tails and the head by hand (23). Under each screen, the review findings pinned where they hit: fixed CR-P3PARA-01, 02, 08 and 10, and 04 in part (Empty. on an ended run fixed, the tail tint still open, so it takes the warning mark); open CR-P3PARA-03, 05, 06, 07, SEC-P3PARA-01 and round 2's CR-P3PARA-09. Middle band, the four round 1 fixes before and after, ac5ae2ca: the name filter q now kept in the URL; All runs goes to /runs, not /runs?state=active; the log tail reads Empty. on an ended run, with a warning that its lines get data-tone but runs.css tints only under .sp-log, so the tail stays plain; Download appends, clicks and removes its anchor, then revokes the URL in a setTimeout of 0. Bottom: two review rounds to scale, one cell per finding, 14 in round 1 and 2 in round 2; round 1 has 7 fixed, 4 in ac5ae2ca, 2 by the simplifier and 1, CR-P3PARA-09, in the docs commit; both rounds approve from code-reviewer and security-reviewer; no blocker, no major; the simplifier took 12 lines out and 8 in; vitest 89 passed, pytest 511 passed. A box lists what is left open after round 2, among it CR-P3PARA-04's log tail tint.](../../assets/diagrams/p3-para-screens_light.png#only-light)

![P3 Task 20: the runs screens, Cancel, and the Playwright smoke. Home, /runs and /runs/id in the Paper shell, refreshed from one stream; ui-spec 4.3, 4.4 and 11; typed read responses, FU-P3-21; inbox 90. Top: spectio serve, whose read routes and :cancel are now typed by api.models in the snapshot openapi/0.0.1.json, sends spectio.run.delta frames on GET /api/v1/events/stream that carry only the run_id; one tab holds one EventSource and shows live, updated N s ago, or Reconnecting; each screen refetches what it shows at most once a second, everything on each reconnection, a new stream 5 s after a refusal, and the run page stops once the run ended (R2 CR-P3T20-05, open: only the latest request wins, so a server slower than 1 s with frames coming never updates; R1 SEC-P3T20-02, open: a store drift would log whole inputs through ResponseValidationError). Four screens: 1, Home at /: counts by state over all runs, the active runs up to 20, the 5 that ended last, the pipeline directories of the last 50 (R2 R3 CR-P3T20-06, open: its race test waits on a list that cannot render, a hidden 1 s sleep). 2, the runs list, /runs: 50 a page, newest first, state and name filters with a 250 ms pause, a frame refetches page 1 and keeps the rows Load more added past it (R1 CR-P3T20-03 fixed: an older answer landed last, now a counter and an AbortController; R1 CR-P3T20-04 fixed: past 200 rows a frame dropped the rest; R3 CR-P3T20-05 open: Load more rows are never refetched). 3, the run, /runs/id: run, processes and stages on a frame for this run until it has ended, stages in order else the processes, an unknown id reads No such run in the shell (R1 CR-P3T20-06, open: two comments disagree on how /runs/%E0 is routed). 4, Cancel behind a confirm: queued or running, not from a terminal; an aria-modal dialog, Keep running has the focus; Cancel the run posts :cancel, 202, Cancelling then Cancelled; the focus moves to the run title (R1 CR-P3T20-01 fixed: focus fell to the page body once Cancel was gone; R1 CR-P3T20-02 fixed: an unread ref dropped). Middle: try it here with tests/e2e/serve-local.sh: a 0700 temp directory, spectio init --local, spectio serve on 127.0.0.1 only; on stop, heads() reads runs/*/launch/.spectio/head.pid (pid pgid start boot host) and names a PID only when head.pid is no symlink and one strict line, pid above 1, pid equal to pgid, this boot by boot_id, alive in /proc with group equal to pid and the same start time; then kill -TERM -- -pid for each head's group, wait up to 10 s, then remove the directory, or keep it and print the PIDs (R2 SEC-P3T20-02 major, fixed in 2f30646: a forged PID 1 line passed and stop() would have run kill -TERM -- -1, every process of the user; R1 SEC-P3T20-01 fixed: Ctrl-C removed the runs root under heads still running; R3 SEC-P3T20-03 open: any group leader of the user, with its real start time; R3 CR-P3T20-07 and 08 open: the comment still claims parity, the grammar is a second copy in shell). CI job e2e-local: Playwright, a real serve, Nextflow from setup-nextflow, desktop and phone, not in ci-ok yet (inbox 90); the 11 tests of skeleton.spec.ts to scale: 5 run always, 3 need Nextflow, 3 fixme, two waiting for sign-in (Task 16) and Snakemake cancel waiting for Task 7 (R1 SEC-P3T20-03 open: the Nextflow download is not checked against NEXTFLOW_SHA256). Bottom: three review rounds, one cell per finding, code-reviewer and security-reviewer in parallel then the simplifier: R1 11 findings, both approve; R2 5, code approve, security changes; R3 5, both approve; no blocker; the simplifier took 2 lines out and 1 in; pytest 511 passed, vitest 63 of 63, before and after; and the list of what is left open after round 3](../../assets/diagrams/p3-t20-runs-screens_light.png#only-light)

![P3 G4: the Paper shell in web/, one shell at three widths. web/src/shell after the G1 boards LiteTopbar, LiteSidebar, MLiteTopbar and MLiteTabbar; ui-spec 7 and 4.11; inbox 89. Top row, widths at half scale; the layout is picked with matchMedia at the first render, so a phone never shows the sidebar for a frame. 1, desktop, 1200 px and up, at /launch: a floating top bar with home, breadcrumbs (Home, Launch) and the theme switch; search, alerts and account wait, disabled, not in 0.0.1; the sidebar open, 216 px, Workspace, Home, Runs, Launch highlighted, server status in its foot, LOCAL · server on login1; the page title Launch. 2, tablet, 768 to 1199 px, the same page, breadcrumbs Home, Launch: the sidebar starts folded to the 72 px rail; a button with aria-expanded unfolds it, and the reader's own fold wins until reload; a warning: the node text is hidden on the rail, R1 CR-P3G4-04. 3, phone, under 768 px: no sidebar, a 64 px tab bar, a 48 px top bar with the node pill, 44 px keys. Middle row, inside the shell. A, server status, in the sidebar foot or the phone pill: spectio serve answers GET /api/v1/version once, at load; Connecting while waiting, login1 online once it answered, not reached once it failed; fixed: R2 CR-P3G4-02, the phone pill was red while connecting, now it turns red only once the call failed, as the sidebar, 7c5737c8. B, breadcrumbs from the path: /runs/run_01K8T2F1XT gives Home, Runs, run_01K8T2F1XT; /runs/%E0 gives Home, Runs, %E0; the last crumb is the page, aria-current; fixed: R1 CR-P3G4-01 major and SEC-P3G4-01, a malformed escape blanked the whole app, now shown as typed, 4e0d2868. C, light and dark: a role=switch aria-checked button named Dark theme; follows the system until pressed, then kept in localStorage; the colour tokens of spectio-tokens.css. Bottom left: 3 review rounds, one cell per finding, code-reviewer and security-reviewer in parallel, then the simplifier: R1, 5 code-reviewer cells, one major, and 1 security cell; every fixed cell is green with a check mark, the R1 major included, CR changes, SEC approve; R2, 4 and 2, both approve, CR-02 and CR-05 and SEC-01 fixed; R3, 1, both approve; IDs restart each round, R1 CR-03 and R2 CR-02 are the same pill fix, R1 CR-01 and SEC-01 the same escape fix; the simplifier changed nothing; R2 CR-05 and SEC-01 are one screenshot fix, fixed after round 3: retaken as alice on login1. Bottom right, left open after round 3, all minor: jsdom prints a scrollTo warning per router render in the tests (R3 CR-P3G4-03); the tablet rail hides the node text from sighted readers (R1 CR-P3G4-04); a long node name ends in an ellipsis, full name only in aria-label (R1 CR-P3G4-05); the phone pill is role=img, so a status change is not announced (R2 CR-P3G4-04); any decoded path segment becomes the page crumb, no limit (R2 SEC-P3G4-02)](../../assets/diagrams/p3-g4-paper-shell_light.png#only-light)

![P3 Task 19: the screens, served beside the API, and the launch form. web/dist at / by spectio_server.static; React, Mantine, TanStack Router; ui-spec 4.6 and 4.11, inbox 88; served only where the API is served. Top row: npm --prefix web ci and npm --prefix web run build, Vite writes hashed assets into web/dist, found beside packages/ until Task 21 ships it in the wheel (SEC-P3T19-01, open: found four levels up from the module, no owner check); spectio serve, where create_app decides once at start, has three outcomes. API mounted and built: the screens at /, /runs, /launch and /runs/id, Home and Runs come with Task 20. API not mounted, a shared host (inbox 86): / is the plain page and says why, /launch and /assets 404. web/dist missing: / says how to build it, the API answers as before, /launch 404. Middle row, what static answers, after the browser checks of Task 17: 4 routes give index.html with Cache-Control no-cache and no other fallback; /assets/name, one plain name of a web type, opened O_NOFOLLOW at each step, regular files only, a FIFO never waits, cached a year, immutable (CR-P3T19-03, fixed: one walk, workflows._open, not a second copy, ae5e988); everything else 404: /index.html, /nope, /launch/x, a dot name, a subfolder, .., a symlinked file or assets directory; every answer a CSP of this origin only, no inline script, nosniff, Host 400, cross-site 403 (SEC-P3T19-03, open: no Referrer-Policy yet). Third row, on /launch in this order: 1, the pipeline: an absolute path, owner/repo, an https or ssh URL, Revision appears only for a repository and is required there (UX-G2-08); 2, Load, then a guess: workflows:inspect reads, never runs, main.nf gives Nextflow, Snakefile or workflow/Snakefile gives Snakemake, both or neither and you choose, aria-pressed buttons (CR-P3T19-01, fixed in 749f621: an edit aborts the inspect and now forgets the guess too); 3, the parameters: one section per $defs group or the top-level properties, no schema gives name and value rows, a value is sent only when set (CR-P3T19-02, fixed in 749f621: an enum of 1 or 2 now sends level: 2, it sent the text and got a 422); 4, Launch, then the run: it stays focusable, aria-disabled, its reason in aria-describedby, a 422 lands on its own field, a 201 from POST /api/v1/runs opens the run page of Task 20. Bottom: two review rounds, one cell per finding, code-reviewer and security-reviewer in parallel then the simplifier: round 1, 9 findings, 3 fixed on the branch, both approve; round 2, no finding, both approve; no blocker, no major; the simplifier took 14 lines out and 8 in, the same 503 tests; CI job web runs npm ci --ignore-scripts, npm test and npm run build, in ci-ok. Left open after round 2: web/dist found four levels up with no owner check (SEC-P3T19-01); the not-built text runs npm ci with its scripts (SEC-P3T19-02); no Referrer-Policy on the screens (SEC-P3T19-03); inbox 88 named a Task 16 TODO in static.py too (CR-P3T19-04); the live shot needs a running serve, by hand (CR-P3T19-05); the Progress section waited for the docs step (CR-P3T19-06). The docs commit closes -04 and -06](../../assets/diagrams/p3-t19-screens_light.png#only-light)

![P3 Task 18: read the runs, tail a log, cancel, watch it live. The /api/v1 runs, tasks, logs and cancel routes and /events/stream, local only, one Unix user, no sign-in yet. Top row, five steps. 1, read the runs: GET /runs newest first, 50 a page, by state and a name prefix, an opaque next cursor; one run with its processes, stages and tasks; 422 for a bad state or a cursor this server did not give, 404 for no run. 2, the end of a log: head-out, head-err or engine, the last 64 KiB; the launch directory comes from the runs root and the run ID and every part is opened O_NOFOLLOW; 404 log-not-found for a missing file, a symlink, a directory that is not private or not a regular file. 3, cancel: POST :cancel on a queued or running run, 202 with the run cancelling, a cancel command for the last launch, the runner writes .spectio/cancel then signals, cancelled once the head ends; 200 unchanged when already cancelling or ended, 422 for a cli-observed run (fixed: SEC-P3T18-01, the event names source api and actor person). 4, every commit noted: a job notes its run and tasks inside its transaction, after the commit they go to the stream, which only records them and never blocks; a failed job sends nothing (fixed: CR-P3T18-01, a new run too). 5, live, once a second: one spectio.run.delta per changed run with its task counts and changed task IDs, a keep-alive after 15 s; 429 rate-limited past 32 streams, a client 64 frames behind is dropped and reconnects (fixed: CR-P3T18-02, SEC-P3T18-02). Middle left: 40 streams opened at once, before the fix all 40 got 200, after it 32 got 200 and 8 got 429. Middle right: every refusal a problem document: a body it cannot parse 422 on body, an unknown path 404, a wrong method 405 about:blank with Allow kept, a chunked body 411, a body over 1 MiB 413, params deeper than 32 and a location over 4096 characters 422 (fixed: SEC-P3T18-04). Bottom: two review rounds, 12 findings then 4, both reviewers approve both times; the gate, 484 passed; and what is still open after round 2](../../assets/diagrams/p3-t18-read-api_light.png#only-light)

![P3 Task 17: inspect a pipeline, then launch it. GET /api/v1/workflows:inspect and POST /api/v1/runs; a pipeline is read, never run, until it is launched. Top row, five steps. 1, every request checked by api.browser before any route: Host 127.0.0.1, localhost or [::1]; no request from another site by Sec-Fetch-Site, Origin or Referer; a write needs Origin and a JSON body; 400 for another host, 403 cross-site, 415 a body that is not JSON (fixed: CR-P3T17-01, SEC-P3T17-01 and -02, DNS rebinding). 2, the location: an absolute path this account reads, or owner/repo, an https or ssh URL; Git needs a revision and is fetched once into data dir/pipelines/host/owner/repo/rev with protocol.allow=never and a 300 s timeout; 422 for credentials in the URL, '..', a control character or a leading dash. 3, read, never run: each path part opened O_NOFOLLOW, a symlink is absent, 1 MiB per file and no YAML aliases; main.nf or Snakefile names the engine, none when both or neither; manifest name and version, profiles, and a schema that is JSON, YAML, inferred or none (fixed: SEC-P3T17-03 and CR-P3T17-03, a linear nextflow.config scan; SEC-P3T17-05 and -08, symlinks). 4, POST /runs checked whole, nothing written before it passes: the location again, the chosen engine's file, one Snakemake profile at most, params against the pipeline's schema; 422 errors with a field and a message; an inferred schema is not enforced (fixed: SEC-P3T17-04, no $ref retrieved). 5, written, then leased: the run directory and manifest, then in one transaction the workflow, version, queued run, params, launch 1 and the launch command the runner leases; 201 and a Location header; 422 on workflow.engine for a second engine on one location. Middle: before all this, create_app decides once at start whether the API is mounted: on one person's machine with executor local it is; it looks shared when the executor is not local, SLURM_CONF is set, /etc/slurm/slurm.conf exists, sinfo is on PATH, or /proc shows a process of another person with uid 1000 or above, not 65534 (best effort, hidepid hides them); then every /api/v1 path answers 404 with the reason and the start text says the same; one scan feeds both (fixed: CR-P3T17-04). Bottom: five review rounds, one cell per finding: R1 14 (2 blockers, 4 majors), code-reviewer changes, security-reviewer block; R2 7, changes and approve; R3 7, approve and changes; R4 7 and R5 4, both approve. Still open after round 5: no clone size cap (inbox #86 follow-up); the shared-host check runs once at start (SEC-P3T17-07 r4); a schema whose $ref points at itself is a 500 (SEC-P3T17-08 r3); a read error after the open is a 500 (SEC-P3T17-09 r5); serving it at all before sign-in is Thomas's reserved decision (inbox #86)](../../assets/diagrams/p3-t17-launch_light.png#only-light)

## Why

- **[P2 §10.1](../../plans/P2-execution.md#101-g1-paper-chrome-on-the-shared-lite-board-components):** G1 restyles the four shared lite components before Task 19, so the 30 lite boards and the code of G4 share one shell.
- **The look:** the GO of 2026-10-29 ([decision log](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-29-go-for-the-whole-p1-plan-with-five-changes), change 5) makes depictio's Paper chrome Spectio's look ([ui-spec §7](../../specs/ui-spec.md#7-consistency-with-depictio-reuse-plan)).
- **[P1 Task 17](../../plans/P1-walking-skeleton.md#task-17-launch-api):** the launch form of Task 19 needs an endpoint that reads a pipeline without running it, and one that launches it through the runner command of Task 9. Built in the local-only order of [inbox #80](../../reviews/inbox.md#80-assumed-a-visible-slice-earlier-in-the-p1-order) (a), before sign-in (Tasks 12 to 16), so the first launch from a browser works sooner on one person's machine. The rules that order needs are in [inbox #86](../../reviews/inbox.md#86-assumed-the-001-launch-api-served-in-local-mode-only-before-sign-in).
- **A pipeline is untrusted input.** A location may be a Git repository someone else wrote ([api §5, Imports are untrusted input](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#imports-are-untrusted-input)). Inspect fetches and reads it but never runs it, and every file it reads must stay inside the pipeline directory.
- **[P1 Task 18](../../plans/P1-walking-skeleton.md#task-18-read-api-logs-and-cancel):** the shell, the runs list and the run detail of Tasks 19 and 20 read runs, tasks and logs, cancel a run, and refresh live over SSE ([api §8.3](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#83-live-streams-sse), decided 2026-10-28). Built before sign-in like Task 17, with the rules of [inbox #87](../../reviews/inbox.md#87-assumed-the-001-read-api-logs-cancel-and-stream-in-the-local-only-order). Three follow-ups were due with it: FU-P3-14 (the Snakemake spool grace), FU-P3-17 (API input hardening) and FU-P3-19 (API test hygiene).
- **[P1 Task 19](../../plans/P1-walking-skeleton.md#task-19-ui-shell-and-launch-form):** the first screen a person uses. The form reads a pipeline through Task 17's inspect and launches it through `POST /runs`; the shell names the server's node from Task 18's `/version`. Built before sign-in like Tasks 17 and 18, so the screens follow the API's rule of [inbox #86](../../reviews/inbox.md#86-assumed-the-001-launch-api-served-in-local-mode-only-before-sign-in): served only where the API is. Its own rules are [inbox #88](../../reviews/inbox.md#88-assumed-the-001-screens-and-launch-form-in-the-local-only-order). G2 left three board items to it: UX-G2-07, -08 and -17.
- **[P2 §10.5](../../plans/P2-execution.md#105-g4-the-paper-shell-in-web):** G4 replaces Task 19's thin shell with the Paper shell in Spectio's own code, light and dark, before Task 20 adds Home, the runs list and the run detail inside it. P1 Assumption 24 asks for the Paper look; no task of P1 builds it ([P2 §11](../../plans/P2-execution.md#11-notes-for-p1) note 3). Its rules are [inbox #89](../../reviews/inbox.md#89-assumed-the-paper-shell-in-web-g4). It also took SEC-P3T19-03 from follow-up FU-P3-22, due before Task 20.
- **[P1 Task 20](../../plans/P1-walking-skeleton.md#task-20-runs-list-run-detail-cancel-and-the-playwright-smoke):** the screens a person watches a run on: Home, the runs list and the run detail with Cancel, inside G4's shell, refreshed from Task 18's stream; and the Playwright smoke that drives them against a real `spectio serve`. Built before sign-in like Tasks 17 to 19, with the rules of [inbox #90](../../reviews/inbox.md#90-assumed-the-001-runs-screens-cancel-and-the-playwright-smoke-in-the-local-only-order). Items owned by Task 20 from earlier steps: FU-P3-21 (a typed OpenAPI document), the `HEAD` and `--ignore-scripts` items of FU-P3-22, the `scrollTo` noise of FU-P3-23, SEC-P3G4-02 (the last crumb shows URL text) and the default "Not Found" body of a malformed run URL (G4 open point 3).
- **UI parity A:** Thomas looked at the screenshots of the 0.0.1 screens and said that many defined things were missing: collapsible sections with small light icons, and the engine logos (2026-10-31, `.autopilot/journal.md`). The `ux-reviewer` [parity audit](../../reviews/ui-parity-0.0.1.md) compared each screen with its lite board and the 0.0.1 API, and listed 34 findings, each marked fix, later in 0.0.1, or leave out. Step A takes the shell, Home, the runs list and the run detail; step B takes the phone and Launch. The mark rule is [inbox #91](../../reviews/inbox.md#91-assumed-which-mark-a-pipeline-shows-no-nf-core-mark-yet-and-where-the-logos-come-from).
- **UI parity B:** the second half of the parity work that Thomas asked for on 2026-10-31 ("continue until it is settled", quoted in the step's prompt; `.autopilot/journal.md` names step B as next). It takes the phone (UX-PAR-08, 09, 10, 34) and the Launch form (UX-PAR-30 to 33) of the [audit](../../reviews/ui-parity-0.0.1.md), and the UI items of follow-up FU-P3-25 (CR-P3PARA-03, 06, 07, 09 r2, 10 r2). It also looks at what the UI parity A retake showed: the Head fact read "Alive" on runs that had ended (journal, "To be checked in step B"). It is not a P1 plan task; the boards (`Lite*`, `MLite*`) are the target, and the exit check is the step's own: every finding fixed or moved with an owner, the tests green, and new live shots that match the boards by eye. The step's prompt lifted the two-round ceiling of `review-code` for this fix.
- **UI parity C:** after UI parity B, Thomas wrote (2026-10-31, the step's prompt): "oui ajoute cela ; mais avant corrige les points d'attention > disposition des params (pk une grille au lieu de 1 par ligne ? ) ; pas de light icons pour les sections dans launch ; run detail UI? je veux les logos, important ; ouverture et consultation des logs ?". So the step takes five points, before the item he agreed to add: the parameters one per line, not in a grid; a light icon on every Launch section; the run detail against its boards (LiteRunDetail, LiteRunActions); the logos, which answers [inbox #91](../../reviews/inbox.md#91-assumed-which-mark-a-pipeline-shows-no-nf-core-mark-yet-and-where-the-logos-come-from) with (b) ([decision log](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-31-ui-parity-c-parameters-one-per-line-the-logos-the-runs-copy-menu-and-logs-written-into-ui-spec-43-44-and-46-p3-parity-c-inbox-91)); and opening and reading the logs (LiteRunLogs). It is not a P1 plan task; the boards are the target.
- **Public demo:** Thomas had looked at the screens through a private page of recorded answers, then asked for them on the real site (2026-10-31, the step's brief): "pourquoi un artifact ? vrai site ?", then "oui ajoute cela". So anyone can click through the real 0.0.1 screens without a clone, on demo data only. It is not a P1 plan task. The [decision log](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-31-a-public-read-only-demo-of-the-001-screens-at-demo-written-into-dev-env-73-p3-demo) records it; publishing is his choice, so its PR is not auto-merged.
- **UI parity D:** after clicking through the public demo preview of PR #111, Thomas wrote (2026-10-31): "plein de probl, steps & stages pas clickable, pas de détails ; plein de parties et d'éléments pas encore clickable ; params quasi vides pour les wf ; pas de wf par défaut de test dans launch pour tester ; logs pas searchable ; pas de logs et info/outputs/err/out par process et task". What the 0.0.1 API serves went to this step; each task's files, exit code and resources need the API and go to P1 Tasks 28, 29 and 32. He said "go" for this order: UI parity D, then P1 Tasks 27, 28, 29, 32, then Task 7 ([decision log](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-31-ui-parity-d-run-detail-sheets-searchable-logs-test-pipelines-in-launch-written-into-ui-spec-44-and-46-and-dev-env-101-p3-parity-d)). It is not a P1 plan task; the boards (LiteRunDetail, LiteRunTasks, LiteRunLogs, LiteTaskFiles, MLiteTaskFilesSheet, LiteLaunch) are the target, and the step had at most two review rounds.
- **The placeholders:** Thomas answered [inbox #72](../../reviews/inbox.md#72-assumed-the-paper-shell-in-001-leaves-out-search-notifications-and-the-avatar) with (b). Search, notifications and the avatar are shown as disabled placeholders, not left out (follow-up FU-P3-02).

## What changed

### G1 · Paper chrome on the shared lite-board components

Three of the four shared components in `design/ui-canvas/` now draw the Paper shell of [ui-spec §7](../../specs/ui-spec.md#7-consistency-with-depictio-reuse-plan). Thirty lite boards import them, and none of the boards was edited: the components keep their props (`crumbs`, `active`, `title`, `sub`, `back`).

- **`LiteTopbar`** (floating top bar):
    - Breadcrumbs: the `crumbs` prop ("Runs / rnaseq-2026-10-25 / Tasks") becomes `Home › Runs › rnaseq-2026-10-25 › Tasks` in a `<nav aria-label="Breadcrumb"><ol>`. Every crumb but the last is a link to its board; the last has `aria-current="page"`. Below 1000 px, the crumbs between Runs and the last fold into "…", the last crumb ellipsizes, and the search box becomes an icon button. A crumb with no board is plain text.
    - Theme toggle: `<button role="switch" aria-checked="false" aria-label="Dark theme">`.
    - Search (⌘K), notifications and the account avatar, as Thomas answered [inbox #72](../../reviews/inbox.md#72-assumed-the-paper-shell-in-001-leaves-out-search-notifications-and-the-avatar) (b): native `<button disabled>` controls named "Search (not in 0.0.1)", "Notifications (not in 0.0.1)" and "Account (not in 0.0.1)". They are dimmed and never take focus. The search box also shows "Search (not in 0.0.1)" as text.
    - The "SLURM · server on login1" chip moved to the sidebar foot.
- **`LiteSidebar`** (foldable sidebar):
    - A "WORKSPACE" eyebrow and a fold button, `<button aria-expanded aria-controls="lite-nav-items">`.
    - A new prop, `collapsed`, draws the folded 92 px icon rail, with the server status as "✓ on". The labels stay readable by a screen reader.
    - The foot shows the server status: "Spectio 0.0.1 · online", then "SLURM · server on login1 · your account amartin".
    - The Runs count reads "Runs, 6 active runs" through visually hidden text.
- **`MLiteTopbar`** (phone): the same controls in the 48 px row: the server chip, the disabled search, notifications and account buttons, and the theme switch (44 px). Below 390 px the search and notifications placeholders are hidden, so the row fits at 320 px.
- **`MLiteTabbar`**: unchanged. It already was the floating bottom tab bar of Paper.

### G2 · The launch boards: engine buttons and path-or-Git

**What changed.** `LiteLaunch`, `LiteLaunchSnakemake` and `MLiteLaunch` in `design/ui-canvas/` now draw the launch form of [ui-spec §4.6](../../specs/ui-spec.md#46-launch-and-relaunch) (note 2026-10-29) and [P1](../../plans/P1-walking-skeleton.md) Assumption 2:

- **Page head** (desktop), as in [ui-spec §7](../../specs/ui-spec.md#7-consistency-with-depictio-reuse-plan): a 48 px icon tile with the sidebar's Launch tint, "Launch a workflow" in the Outfit heading font, one line of description, and Launch on the right. A second Launch ends the aside, which stays in view from 1200 px; both share one state, and when disabled both show the reason ("Fill the required field: samples").
- **Pipeline:** one field takes an absolute path, a GitHub or nf-core name, or an `https://` or `ssh` URL. Beside it are **Revision (Git only)** and Load. For a path, Revision is disabled and says why. `LiteLaunchSnakemake` shows a Git repository at a revision: Revision is required, and a short name is read as GitHub before Load ("Read as GitHub: https://github.com/…").
- **Engine:** two native `<button aria-pressed>`, Nextflow and Snakemake, in a group labelled "Engine". The guessed one is pressed and shows a check mark. "Guessed from main.nf · change it if the guess is wrong" sits beside them; on a prefilled launch it reads "Nextflow, as in rnaseq-2026-10-25".
- **States**, drawn as cards at the foot of each board:
    - no guess, both files found (`LiteLaunch`, `MLiteLaunch`): no button is pressed and Launch waits for a choice;
    - no guess, neither file found (`LiteLaunchSnakemake`): Launch stays disabled with "No main.nf or Snakefile in this directory: check the path", whatever is pressed;
    - guess overruled (`LiteLaunch`): Snakemake pressed on a directory with no Snakefile, so Launch says it will refuse;
    - Git (`LiteLaunchSnakemake`): before Load with no revision, fetching, and not fetched (revision not found, with the other fetch failures listed);
    - refused input, following [api §5](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#imports-are-untrusted-input): a `file://` URL (`LiteLaunch`, `MLiteLaunch`) and a revision that starts with `-` (`LiteLaunchSnakemake`).

### G3a · Paper page bodies of the run boards

The run boards and their phone twins now open with the page head of [ui-spec §7](../../specs/ui-spec.md#7-consistency-with-depictio-reuse-plan): a tinted 48 px icon tile, the Outfit h1, a one-line description and the primary action on the right. `LiteHome` and `LiteRuns` already had it and are unchanged.

- **Run heads** (`LiteRunDetail`, `LiteRunDetailSnakemake`, `LiteRunObserved`, and the page behind `LiteCancelDialog`):
    - the pipeline logo sits in a 48 px tile with the Runs tint (`--sp-violet-50`), in place of a white bordered 52 px tile;
    - the run name wraps instead of overflowing, and the state line wraps as one sentence;
    - Cancel run and Copy form one group on the right. The page behind the cancel dialog shows Copy too.
- **States boards** (`LiteStates`, `LiteStatesRun`): a tile in the Runs tint and a one-line description. The rest of the old description is a caption under the panels. The run-detail loading skeleton draws the tile too.
- **Phone, top-level pages:**
    - `MLiteRuns` keeps its title and "live, updated" line in the compact top bar, as ui-spec §4.11 asks; it is unchanged.
    - `MLiteHome` already had its h1 in the body. It gains a tile in `--sp-indigo-100`, the `-100` step because the phone page is grey.
    - Its description now reads "4 running, 1 queued, 1 cancelling · live, updated 4 s ago", shortened from "4 runs running, 1 queued and 1 cancelling" to fit beside the tile.
- **Phone, run pages** (`MLiteRunDetail`, `MLiteRunObserved`, `MLiteCancelSheet`):
    - the back link, h1 and state line stay in the top bar;
    - the white run card starts with the pipeline in a 48 px tile (`--sp-violet-50`);
    - the card behind the cancel sheet shows Copy too.
- **Two G1 minors:**
    - UX-G1-12, `LiteTopbar`: below 1000 px, only the crumbs before the run fold. The link back to the run stays: "Home › … › rnaseq-2026-10-25 › Tasks".
    - UX-G1-13, `MLiteTopbar`: the search and notifications placeholders are 44 px wide, and the row gap is 2 px.
- **`crumbs` props** (UX-G1-11): no G3a board names an in-page tab in its crumbs. `LiteTaskFilesStates` does ("… / Tasks"); it is a G3b board.

### Task 17 · Launch API

Branch `autopilot/P3-t17`, base `643dd89`, head `3cbf735` before the figure and this page; 7 commits, 25 files, +2480 −112 (+2132 −112 in `packages/`, +308 in `uv.lock`). The endpoints are owned by the [api §5 note of 2026-10-30](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#5-endpoint-catalogue-api-v1) and [Imports are untrusted input](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#imports-are-untrusted-input); the browser checks by [dev-env §15.3](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#153-001-on-a-cluster-architecture-and-safety-without-accounts) req. 4 and 7; the rules assumed for the local-only order by [inbox #86](../../reviews/inbox.md#86-assumed-the-001-launch-api-served-in-local-mode-only-before-sign-in). This list names the files.

- **The API** (`packages/spectio-server/src/spectio_server/api/`; new dependencies `jsonschema>=4.23,<5` and `pyyaml>=6.0.2,<7`):
    - `workflows.py` (444 lines): `GET /api/v1/workflows:inspect?location=&revision=`.
        - `resolve` checks the location: an absolute path to a directory this account can read, or Git (`owner/repo`, `https://` or `ssh://`), under `git_url` and `check_revision`.
        - `fetch` clones a Git location once per revision into `<data dir>/pipelines/<host>/<path…>/<revision>/`, under a temporary name then renamed. `git` runs as an argument list with `-c protocol.allow=never` and only `https` and `ssh` turned back on, no terminal, `GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0`, and a 300 s timeout that kills its process group.
        - `inspect_dir` reads the directory and never runs it: `entry_files`, the engine guess, the Nextflow manifest name and version, the profiles, and the schema with its source. Every file is opened one path part at a time from a descriptor of the pipeline directory, with `O_NOFOLLOW` (`_open`, `_regular`, `_read`), and only a regular file of at most 1 MiB is read. YAML aliases are refused. `nextflow.config` is scanned in linear time and never run.
        - `engine_versions`: both engines' versions from `check_engine`, on each inspect.
    - `runs_write.py` (207 lines): `POST /api/v1/runs`. It checks the whole body before writing anything: the location as inspect does, the chosen engine's entry file, the profiles, the name, and the params against the pipeline's schema (`jsonschema`, in the draft its `$schema` names, draft 7 when there is none: `validator_for(schema, default=Draft7Validator)`; an empty `referencing.Registry`, so no `$ref` is retrieved). Then the run directory and its manifest, then the rows and the `launch` command in one transaction. `201` with a `Location` header.
    - `browser.py` (66 lines): one middleware before every route. Host allow-list (`400`), cross-site refusal (`403`), and on writes `Origin` (`403`) and a JSON body (`415`).
    - `problems.py` (57 lines): `422 validation-failed` with `errors: [{field, message}]`, `field` a dotted path; pydantic's echoed `input` is dropped.
- **The server:**
    - `app.py`: `create_app(settings, writer)` mounts the two routers only when `no_api_reason(settings)` is `None`: kind `local`, and `shared_host()` finds no sign of a cluster node or a shared machine. Otherwise every `/api/v1` path answers `404` with the reason. The decision is taken once and kept on `app.state.no_api`.
    - `serve.py`: `Started.no_api` carries that decision, and `start_text(settings, node, user, no_api)` prints it.
    - `store/runs.py` (123 lines): `insert_run` and `first`, shared by `POST /runs` and the rebuild (one engine per location, api §7).
    - `rebuild.py`: uses `insert_run`, and refuses a Git location that fails the import rule (SEC-P3T11-01 (r3)). A Git manifest with no revision is still accepted until follow-up FU-P3-15 (CR-P3T17-05 (r1)).
    - `sink.py`: `active_launches` keeps a launch until its head is reported ended, also after its run ended (CR-P3T09-R5-03).
- **Tests:** `tests/api/test_launch.py` (30 tests, 58 cases), `tests/api/test_req4_req7_browser.py` (7 tests), `tests/security/test_shared_host.py` (6 tests), 4 new tests in `tests/test_serve.py`, and four schema fixtures in `tests/data/`. Dev dependencies `httpx>=0.27`, `types-jsonschema`, `types-pyyaml`.
- **Specs** (P1 Process: the owning spec changes in the same PR):
    - [api §5](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#5-endpoint-catalogue-api-v1): the dated note of 2026-10-30 (the worker's commit). The docs commit adds two bullets: a symlinked file counts as absent, and where the API is not served (CR-P3T17-06 (r4)).
    - [dev-env §15.3](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#153-001-on-a-cluster-architecture-and-safety-without-accounts) req. 4 and req. 7: dated notes (docs commit). Both requirements are enforced from Task 17, and the cross-site stand-in for the session cookie fails open for a `GET`.
    - Each is marked *(assumed — pending Thomas, inbox #86)*. No section number changed.
- **Inbox:** #86 new (worker), with its residual-risk text completed and an option (d) added by the docs commit (SEC-P3T17-07 (r2), SEC-P3T17-07 (r4), CR-P3T17-06 (r2)).
- **Decision log:** [2026-10-30 — the 0.0.1 launch API](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-30-the-001-launch-api-written-into-api-5-and-dev-env-153-p3-task-17-inbox-86).
- **Review record:** [`docs/reviews/code/P3-t17.md`](../../reviews/code/P3-t17.md).
- **Docs of this task:** this section, the `p3-t17-launch` figure (`tools/diagrams/p3_t17_launch.py`, by `diagram-artist`, commit `b8c75e9`) and its entry in `docs/assets/media.yaml`.

### Task 18 · Read API, logs, cancel and the stream

Branch `autopilot/P3-t18`, base `87aaa59`, head `562510b` before the figure and this page; 6 commits, 30 files, +2823 −72 (+2763 −47 in `packages/`, +37 −25 in `uv.lock` and `pyproject.toml`, +23 in the inbox). The endpoints are owned by the [api §5 note of Task 18](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#5-endpoint-catalogue-api-v1), the stream by [api §8.3](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#83-live-streams-sse), the new problem slugs by [api §4.1](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#41-wire-conventions); the rules assumed for the local-only order by [inbox #87](../../reviews/inbox.md#87-assumed-the-001-read-api-logs-cancel-and-stream-in-the-local-only-order). This list names the files.

- **The API** (`packages/spectio-server/src/spectio_server/api/`):
    - `runs_read.py` (378 lines): `GET /runs`, `/runs:summary`, `/runs/{id}`, `/runs/{id}/processes`, `/stages`, `/tasks` and `/version`.
        - The run carries `workflow`, `head`, `task_counts` and `summary.tasks` (`done`, `total`, `failed`, `cached`), as api §7 names them, not as P1 does (#87 rule 3).
        - Lists are `{items, next}`; `next` is base64url of `[sort value, id]`. A cursor this server did not give, a naive time included (`ec75d07`), is `422` on `cursor`.
        - Each read opens its own connection on the writer's engine, so a reader never waits for the writer (SQLite WAL).
    - `logs.py` (95 lines): `GET /runs/{id}/logs?source=head-out|head-err|engine`, the last 64 KiB. The launch directory comes from the runs root and the run ID and must be private (`rundir.private_dir`); each part below it is opened with `O_NOFOLLOW`, and only a regular file is read. Anything else is `404 log-not-found`.
    - `cancel.py` (69 lines): `POST /runs/{id}:cancel`. `queued` or `running` → `cancelling`, a `cancel` command for the last launch, `202`; otherwise `200` unchanged; `422 not-in-this-version` for a `cli-observed` run. The event names `source: api`, `actor_kind: person` and the principal (SEC-P3T18-01 (r1)).
    - `stream.py` (164 lines): `GET /events/stream`. `Stream.notify` records changes from the writer thread under a lock; a pump task sends one `spectio.run.delta` per changed run per second; 64 frames per client queue; at most 32 open connections, counted from the handler's check to the end of the response (`Stream.open`, `Stream.close`, `_Response`; CR-P3T18-02, SEC-P3T18-02 (r1)).
    - `problems.py`: a handler for Starlette's `HTTPException`: a body-parse 400 becomes `422 validation-failed` on `body`, 404 becomes `not-found`, any other status `about:blank` with its headers (SEC-P3T18-04 (r1)).
    - `browser.py`, `workflows.py`, `runs_write.py`: the FU-P3-17 hardening (`4bddad3`): `411` and `413` on writes, a malformed `Referer` is `403`, a location over 4096 characters or refused by the file system is `422`, params nest 32 levels at most, a schema that cannot be used is `422` on `params`, a read error after the open is `422`, and `refuse_control` also refuses Unicode categories Cf, Zl and Zp.
- **The store and the server:**
    - `store/writer.py`: `note_change(conn, run_id, task_ids)` records changes in the transaction; after the commit the writer thread hands them to `on_commit`. A failed job hands nothing.
    - `projector/runs.py`: `Projection` collects the tasks it creates or moves and notes them at `finish`; `move` takes the event's columns. `store/runs.py`: `insert_run` notes the new run (CR-P3T18-01 (r1)) and stores `workflow_version`.
    - migration `0004_read_api.py`: `run_stages` (without `gate_id`), `tasks.stage_id`, `workflow_versions.version` (#87 rules 4 and 5).
    - `ids.py`: IDs are monotonic within the process, so tasks sort by ID in first-seen order (#87 rule 11).
    - `app.py`: the stream is built and wired to the writer, and the six routers are mounted, only when `no_api` is `None`; `NO_API` no longer names the Host checks. `serve.py`: uvicorn's access log is off, and a stop waits at most 5 s for open streams.
    - `openapi/0.0.1.json`: the OpenAPI document, 765 lines, 12 paths.
- **The runner** (`packages/spectio/src/spectio/runner/loop.py`, `df61c4e`): a Snakemake head that exits with a code ends its launch at the poll that sees it, after one more spool read, not 60 s later (FU-P3-14, CR-P3T09-04).
- **Tests:** `tests/api/test_read.py` (17 tests, 21 cases), `test_cancel.py` (4), `test_stream.py` (8), `test_openapi.py` (2), `test_hardening.py` (10 tests, 19 cases), and `test_snakemake_end_skips_the_spool_grace` in `packages/spectio/tests/test_local_launcher.py`. `conftest.py` gains the `client` fixture (a machine that looks shared), `add_run`, `replay` (a T2 fixture through the real spool reader), `replayed_flaky` and `seeded_stages`; `test_stream.py` runs a real uvicorn server (`served`). The dev dependency `httpx2>=2.16,<3` replaces `httpx` (FU-P3-19).
- **Specs** (P1 Process: the owning spec changes in the same PR), all three by the docs commit, marked *(assumed — pending Thomas, inbox #87)*; no section number changed:
    - [api §4.1](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#41-wire-conventions), note 2026-10-30: `length-required` (411), `request-too-large` (413), and the framework's refusals;
    - [api §5](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#5-endpoint-catalogue-api-v1), note of Task 18: the run's shape, lists and cursors, `/version`, the log tail, `:cancel`;
    - [api §8.3](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#83-live-streams-sse), note 2026-10-30: the 0.0.1 stream.
- **Inbox:** #87 new (worker, `8536932`); rules 12 (framework refusals) and 13 (who cancelled) added by the docs commit (CR-P3T18-03 (r2)).
- **Decision log:** [2026-10-30 — the 0.0.1 read API](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-30-the-001-read-api-logs-cancel-and-stream-written-into-api-41-5-and-83-p3-task-18-inbox-87).
- **Review record:** [`docs/reviews/code/P3-t18.md`](../../reviews/code/P3-t18.md).
- **Docs of this task:** this section, the `p3-t18-read-api` figure (`tools/diagrams/p3_t18_read_api.py`, by `diagram-artist`, commit `f64c27d`) and its entry in `docs/assets/media.yaml`.

#### Differences from the plan's interface (Task 18)

[P1 Task 18](../../plans/P1-walking-skeleton.md#task-18-read-api-logs-and-cancel) gives the interface. The code differs as follows; inbox #87 holds the rules. P1's text changes in the next `/apply-findings` pass.

| Plan | Code | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Depends on 4, 5, 17; `/version` and the stream behind the session cookie | built before 12 to 16; `test_version_behind_session` and `test_stream_needs_session` assert `404` off local mode, `400` for a foreign Host, `403` cross-site, each with a `TODO(P1 Task 16)` | #87 rules 1 and 2 |
| `summary: {tasks_total, tasks_expected, counts}` | `summary: {tasks: {done, total, failed, cached}, last_failure}`, and `task_counts` on the run | api §7; no `tasks_total` column (#78); #87 rule 3 |
| `workflow.version` from the bridge's start event | the `manifest.version` inspect read at launch, in `workflow_versions.version` (migration 0004) | nf-spectio's start event carries no version; #87 rule 4, option (b) not taken |
| `engine_version` from `check_engine` at launch | `runs.engine_version`, pinned from the start event | the store keeps no launch-time value; #87 rule 4 |
| logs: a real-path check, then `O_NOFOLLOW` | an `O_NOFOLLOW` walk from a descriptor of the private launch directory | the walk is the real-path check, made at open time; #87 rule 9 |
| Files: `api/{runs_read,logs,cancel,stream}.py`, `openapi/0.0.1.json` | also `store/writer.py`, migration 0004, `ids.py`, `problems.py`, the FU-P3-17 files and `loop.py` | the stream needs a writer hook; first-seen order needs monotonic IDs; three follow-ups were due at Task 18 |
| the stream: one frame per change, at most one per run per second, `: keep-alive` every 15 s | as planned, plus 64 frames per client, 32 open connections, no `id:` and no replay | #87 rule 7 |
| tests: 17 named | 41 tests, 54 cases in five files, and one in `test_local_launcher.py` | one test per review fix and per FU-P3-17 refusal |

### Task 19 · UI shell and launch form

Branch `autopilot/P3-t19`, base `0713a1e`, head `ae5e988` before the figure and this page; 4 commits, 32 files, +5342 −32. Of these, `web/` is +4994 (+3595 in `package-lock.json`), `packages/` +246 −27, `tools/media/` +49 −4, `ci.yml` +23 −1, the inbox and the media manifest +26. How the screens are served is owned by [dev-env §15.3 req. 17](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#153-001-on-a-cluster-architecture-and-safety-without-accounts), the form by the [ui-spec §4.6 note of 2026-10-30](../../specs/ui-spec.md#46-launch-and-relaunch), and the rules assumed for the local-only order by [inbox #88](../../reviews/inbox.md#88-assumed-the-001-screens-and-launch-form-in-the-local-only-order). This list names the files.

- **The server** (`packages/spectio-server/src/spectio_server/`):
    - `static.py` (80 lines, new): `install(app, dist)` adds `GET` on `/`, `/runs`, `/launch` and `/runs/{run_id}`, which answer `index.html`, and `GET /assets/{name}`, which answers one file. `read(root, *parts)` opens through `workflows._open` (`O_NOFOLLOW` at each step, `root` included) and returns a regular file or `None`. The headers are in `CSP` and `HEADERS`. `DIST` is `web/dist` four levels up from the module (`TODO(P1 Task 21)`).
    - `app.py`: `create_app(settings, writer, web_dir=static.DIST)`. When `no_api` is `None` and `static.built(web_dir)`, it installs the screens. Otherwise `/` stays a plain-text page (now out of the OpenAPI schema) that gives the `no_api` reason or `NOT_BUILT`. `TODO(P1 Task 16)`: behind the session.
    - `openapi/0.0.1.json`: the plain `/` page leaves the snapshot (−18 lines).
- **The screens** (`web/`, new; every version exact in `package.json`, `package-lock.json` committed; #88 rule 5):
    - `routes.tsx`: a thin shell, a top bar with "Spectio 0.0.1 · server on <node>" from `GET /version`, side links, and a tab bar on the phone. It has four routes. `/`, `/runs` and `/runs/$id` say "This screen comes with P1 Task 20."; G4 replaces the shell with the Paper shell.
    - `api.ts`: a hand-written client for `workflows:inspect`, `POST /runs` and `GET /version`, and `ApiError` with the `422`'s `errors` (#88 rule 4).
    - `launch/LaunchPage.tsx` (505 lines): the form of the ui-spec §4.6 note. Pipeline, Revision (only for a repository), Load (an edit aborts it), Engine, Profile, Run name, the parameters, and an aside "Where it runs" (executor, node, engine and version, run directory) that ends with Launch. On a `201` it opens `/runs/<id>`.
    - `launch/EngineButtons.tsx`: two native `<button aria-pressed>` in a group labelled "Engine" (UX-G2-07). `launch/guessEngine.ts`: G5's `guessEngine(files)`.
    - `launch/schemaFields.ts`: `schemaFields(schema)` returns `Section[]`, one per `$defs` or `definitions` group or one for top-level properties. Hidden fields are skipped, and `Field.options` keeps the raw enum values (CR-P3T19-02).
    - `app.css`: the layout. The aside is sticky from 1200 px, at most the window's height, with its own scroll (UX-G2-17). Below 768 px the launch bar is pinned above the tab bar.
    - `vite.config.ts`: `assetsInlineLimit: 0`, so the build has no inline script for the CSP to refuse.
- **CI:** job `web` in `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: the Node of `dev/versions.env`, `npm ci --no-audit --no-fund --ignore-scripts`, `npm test`, `npm run build`. It feeds `ci-ok`. `.gitignore` gains `web/node_modules/` and `web/dist/`.
- **Media:** `tools/media/live.js` (new) captures a page of a running `spectio serve` with Playwright, after `fill:`, `click:` and `wait:` steps. `shots.py` gains the `live` field and the `app-launch` shot, which it skips without `SPECTIO_URL`. The page text gets the same hygiene check as the boards, without rewriting. The manifest entry `app-launch` and the `tools/media/README.md` paragraph go with it.
- **Tests:** `packages/spectio-server/tests/api/test_web.py` (10 tests, 19 cases); `conftest.py`'s `client_for` takes `web_dir`, which is none by default. `web/src/`: 4 files, 17 tests (`schemaFields` 4, `guessEngine` 1, `EngineButtons` 3, `LaunchPage` 9).

Branch `autopilot/P3-t20`, base `b0e2edf`, head `4a70188` before the figure and this page; 8 commits, 46 files, +3892 −115. Of these, `web/` is +2350 −50, `packages/` +1097 −59 (+776 −24 in the OpenAPI snapshot), `tests/e2e/` +330 −2, `ci.yml` +61, `tools/media/` +16 −4, and `docs/` +38 with five shots. The screens are owned by the ui-spec notes of 2026-10-31 in [§4.2](../../specs/ui-spec.md#42-overview), [§4.3](../../specs/ui-spec.md#43-runs-table-and-rows) and [§4.4](../../specs/ui-spec.md#44-run-detail); the typed responses by the [api §5 note of Task 18](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md#5-endpoint-catalogue-api-v1); `HEAD` on the screens by [dev-env §15.3 req. 17](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#153-001-on-a-cluster-architecture-and-safety-without-accounts); the smoke and `serve-local.sh` by [dev-env §6.1](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#61-end-to-end-tests-testse2e), the CI job by [dev-env §11](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#11-ci-workflows); the rules assumed for the local-only order by [inbox #90](../../reviews/inbox.md#90-assumed-the-001-runs-screens-cancel-and-the-playwright-smoke-in-the-local-only-order). This list names the files.

- **The server** (`packages/spectio-server/src/spectio_server/`, `d12a0e2`):
    - `api/models.py` (144 lines, new): pydantic response models for `GET /runs`, `/runs/{id}`, `/runs:summary`, `/version`, `/processes`, `/stages`, `/tasks` and `POST :cancel`. The routes still build dictionaries; FastAPI checks each against its model. The state enums repeat `store.schema`'s, and a test pins them (FU-P3-21).
    - `runs_read.py`: each route names its model; the task cursor is the task ID once, not `[id, id]` (CR-P3T18-05).
    - `app.py`: `API_VERSION = "0.0.1"` is the OpenAPI `info.version`, so a release bump no longer changes the document (CR-P3T18-03). `openapi/0.0.1.json` grows by 776 lines, mostly schemas.
    - `static.py`: the four screen routes and `/assets/<name>` answer `HEAD` (was `405`); the "not built" text says `npm --prefix web ci --ignore-scripts` (SEC-P3T19-02).
- **The screens** (`web/src/`, `f59a374`, `c27bb6f`, `e6dc116`, `4a70188`):
    - `live.tsx` (118 lines): one `EventSource` per browser tab on `/api/v1/events/stream`, shared by every screen. `useFrames(refetch, runId?, enabled)` calls `refetch` at most once a second after a frame (for that run only, when given) and on each (re)connection; after a refusal a new stream opens in 5 s. `LiveStatus` reads "live, updated N s ago", "connecting…" or "Reconnecting…".
    - `home/HomePage.tsx` (161 lines): a greeting, count cards from `GET /runs:summary`, the active runs (up to 20), the five that ended last, and "Your pipelines", up to six distinct locations of the last 50 runs. A load counter drops an older refresh (CR-P3T20-03).
    - `runs/RunsPage.tsx` (213 lines): the table (Run, State, Workflow, Tasks, Started, Duration), five state filters as `aria-pressed` buttons with counts, a name filter sent as `q` after 250 ms, 50 rows a page and Load more, a "From a terminal" chip on `cli-observed` runs, and the "No runs yet." empty state. A filter change aborts the requests in flight; a frame refetches the first page and keeps the rows Load more added past it, and their cursor (CR-P3T20-03, -04).
    - `runs/RunDetailPage.tsx` (458 lines): the header (name, short ID, state word, the stages line, tasks, duration, live status) with Cancel run while `queued` or `running` and not `cli-observed`; a Mantine `Modal` asks "Cancel this run?", with Keep running focused; after `:cancel` the focus moves to the run's `h1` (CR-P3T20-01). Tabs Summary (facts, counts, stages or the flat processes list), Tasks (state and process filters; `failed` first on a failed run), Parameters, and Logs (head out, head err, engine log, loaded on click in `<pre>`). A malformed or unknown ID shows "No such run" in the shell.
    - `runs/stateWord.ts` and `RunState.tsx`: the run, stage and process words of ui-spec §3.1 and the §4.4 note, icon and word together, the short ID, durations and times.
    - `routes.tsx`: the three placeholder pages are replaced; the root route gets a `notFoundComponent`. `shell/crumb.ts` and `TopBar.tsx`: a page sets its own last crumb (the run's name from the API); URL text is never shown (SEC-P3G4-02).
    - `api.ts` (+164): types and calls for the read routes, still hand-written; `api.test.ts` checks ten sample objects against the snapshot's schemas (#90 rule 8). `testing.tsx` (126 lines): shared fetch mocks. `test-setup.ts` stubs `window.scrollTo` (FU-P3-23).
- **The smoke** (`tests/e2e/`, `298ccd6`, `e6dc116`, `2f30646`):
    - `skeleton.spec.ts` (198 lines): the seven tests of P1 Task 20 Step 1, plus a Nextflow cancel, a foreign `Host`, the page headers and a malformed run ID: 11 tests. Three are `test.fixme`: the two session tests (`TODO(P1 Task 16)`) and the Snakemake cancel (`TODO(P1 Task 7)`). Three need `SPECTIO_E2E_ENGINES=1`. `playwright.config.ts` adds the file to the `desktop` and `phone` projects.
    - `serve-local.sh` (130 lines): `spectio init --local` in a fresh 0700 temporary directory, `spectio serve` in the foreground, `--port`; it builds `web/dist` when missing (`--ignore-scripts`) and `nf-spectio` when Nextflow is found. On exit it stops the server, then sends `SIGTERM` to the process group of each live head, waits up to 10 s, and removes the directory; if a head still runs it keeps the directory and prints the PIDs (SEC-P3T20-01 (r1)). `heads()` names a PID only from a strict `head.pid` line that matches a live group leader on this boot (SEC-P3T20-02 (r2)).
- **CI:** job `e2e-local` in `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: Node, uv and `nf-core/setup-nextflow` at the `dev/versions.env` pins, the workspace, `web/dist` and Chromium, then `serve-local.sh --port 8765` and the smoke on both projects with engines on. Report and server log go to the `e2e-local-report` artifact (14 days). It is **not** in `ci-ok`'s `needs` (#90 rule 4).
- **Media:** `live.js` expands `$VARS` in a shot's route; `shots.py` gains `app-home`, `app-runs`, `app-run-detail` and `app-run-detail-phone`, light only; `tools/media/README.md` says what the live shots need (`0cceb8f`).
- **Tests:** `packages/spectio-server/tests/api/`: `test_openapi.py` +3 tests (response models, `info.version`, enums match the store), `test_read.py` +1 (one-ID cursor), `test_web.py` +1 (`HEAD`); `packages/spectio/tests/test_serve_local.py` (2 tests, new). `web/src/`: 5 new test files, 39 new tests (63 in all, 10 files).
- **Not in the task:** `test_stream.py` imports `MAX_CLIENTS` at the top and drops an unused fixture (CR-P3T20-05, round 1: a FU-P3-21 item, kept).
- **Specs** (P1 Process: the owning spec changes in the same PR), all by the docs commit, marked *(assumed — pending Thomas, inbox #90)*; no section number changed:
    - [ui-spec §4.2](../../specs/ui-spec.md#42-overview), [§4.3](../../specs/ui-spec.md#43-runs-table-and-rows) and [§4.4](../../specs/ui-spec.md#44-run-detail), notes 2026-10-31: Home, the list and the run detail as built (#90 rules 5 to 7);

    - [dev-env §6.1](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#61-end-to-end-tests-testse2e) and [§11](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#11-ci-workflows): the smoke, `serve-local.sh`, and the `web` and `e2e-local` jobs (#90 rules 1 to 4). The `web` job of Task 19 was missing from §11.

### UI parity A · The shell, Home, the runs list and the run detail

Branch `autopilot/P3-parA`, base `2785941`, code head `7a4811c` before the figure and this page. It has 5 commits: 36 files, +2387 −440. Of these, `web/` is +2366 −440 in 28 files, `docs/` is +18 (the inbox, the media manifest and five shots), and `tools/media/` is +3. There is no server change and no new dependency.

The step comes from the [UI parity audit](../../reviews/ui-parity-0.0.1.md) of 2026-10-31. It fixes UX-PAR-01 to 07, 11 to 27 and 29, within what the 0.0.1 API serves. Ownership:

- the screens: the ui-spec notes "UI parity A" of 2026-10-31 in [§4.2](../../specs/ui-spec.md#42-overview), [§4.3](../../specs/ui-spec.md#43-runs-table-and-rows), [§4.4](../../specs/ui-spec.md#44-run-detail) and [§7](../../specs/ui-spec.md#7-consistency-with-depictio-reuse-plan);
- which mark a pipeline shows: [inbox #91](../../reviews/inbox.md#91-assumed-which-mark-a-pipeline-shows-no-nf-core-mark-yet-and-where-the-logos-come-from).

This list names the files.

- **The shell** (`web/src/shell/`, `28a241c`):
    - `EngineMark.tsx` (19 lines, new): the Nextflow or Snakemake mark, or nothing for an unknown engine. It never shows the nf-core mark (#91 rule 1). Its `alt` is the engine's name, or empty when the name is written beside it.
    - `FoldSection.tsx` (70 lines, new): the folding section of UX-318. A native button inside the `h2` (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`), a line icon in a tile, a summary shown only while folded, and a chevron. The choice is kept in `localStorage` per screen and section (UX-PAR-02).
    - `PageHead.tsx` (33 lines, new): the 48 px tile in the section's colour, the `h1`, the subtitle and the action (UX-PAR-03). Home, Runs and Launch use it. Launch changes only here (`LaunchPage.tsx`, +6 −7).
    - `Sidebar.tsx` and `TabBar.tsx`: Runs and Launch stay active on their child pages. The Runs item has an active-run badge from `GET /runs:summary`, refreshed on frames, with a visually hidden text for screen readers. The status pill has a check icon (UX-PAR-06, UX-PAR-07). `TopBar.tsx`: crumb links match exactly, so only the last crumb is current.
    - `theme.ts` (+47 −4): the run-state tokens `--sp-state-<tone>`, `-fg` and `-bg` for eight tones, light and dark, copied from `spectio-tokens.css` (UX-PAR-05). Four role tokens are added too: `surface-code`'s text and level tints and `surface-mark`.
    - `shell.css` (+147): the page head, the badge, the fold sections and the dark tile behind a mark.
- **Icons and logos:** `web/src/icons.tsx` (50 lines, new) holds the lucide-style SVG paths that the screens share; the `Icon` component moves there from `Sidebar.tsx`. `web/src/assets/logos/logo-nextflow.png` and `logo-snakemake.png` are byte copies of `docs/assets/logos/` (#91 rule 2); Vite emits them as files.
- **Home** (`home/HomePage.tsx`, 254 lines): the subtitle lists the active states that have runs (UX-PAR-13). Count cards link to `/runs?state=…` and have a detail line (UX-PAR-12). The rows have rails, the engine's mark and a context that is never blank (UX-PAR-14, -16). "Your pipelines" has marks (UX-PAR-15). "Where your runs go" is filled from `GET /version` (UX-PAR-11). Each section folds.
- **The runs list** (`runs/RunsPage.tsx`, 272 lines): `runsSearch` validates `state` and `q` for the route (`routes.tsx`). One `setSearch` writes both with `replace`. The Workflow cell has the mark and the inline version. The Tasks cell says "n failed", "jobs" or "known so far". The subtitle says where heads run.
