#### The Head word after a run ends (UI parity B)

The UI parity A retake showed "Head: Alive" on two runs that had ended. The cause is in the runner, not the screen:

- The API says a head is alive while its launch has no `exited_at` (`runs_read.py:112`).
- The runner reports a head's end only after its end grace of 60 s, because the spool may still hold the run's last events (`runner/loop.py:87` and `:93`, and `_report` at `:272-282`; a Snakemake head with an exit code is drained at once, with no grace).
- So for up to 60 s after a Nextflow run ends, its head reads alive. On the two retake runs it stayed so for longer: the round-1 `code-reviewer` read the store (read-only) and found `exited_at` about 62–63 s after `ended_at` on every earlier run, but set on these two only when the next server adopted them. The server of the retake had stopped inside the grace.

`2f6809b5` reported the end at once, with the run's end after the grace. Round 3 found that this broke the grace: the early report set `launches.exited_at`, the sink stops listing a launch whose run is terminal once `exited_at` is set, so the runner stopped reading that run's spool inside the grace (CR-P3PARB-01 r3, major). `7993752a` reverts it whole. The screen now says **Ending** for a run that has ended while its head's end is not reported yet (`RunHeader.tsx`, `headWord`, `614b81f`), and the folded facts read "head ending". "Open the head by hand" still shows for such a run, since its head may really be alive.

This list names the files.

- **The Launch form** (`eb46400`, `ae60a73`, `089adc3`):

Each `ParamField` passes Mantine an `inputContainer` that draws the meta (type, format, "changed", the default), then the input, then the help, and hides Mantine's own description visually (`sp-sr`). The visible meta and help are `aria-hidden`, so assistive technology hears the description once, "help · meta". A boolean is drawn by hand in the same row, its description tied by `aria-describedby` (CR-P3PARC-01, `ae60a73`).
    - The Pipeline section is a `FoldSection` with the route icon, above the two-column grid; Parameters (sliders), Where it runs (server) and the preview (file-code) are `FoldSection`s too. The preview card gets the dark code surface (`launch-preview-card`).

- **The marks** (`eb46400`): `shell/EngineMark.tsx` gains `markFor(engine, name)`: the nf-core mark when `name` starts with `nf-core/` (case-sensitive), else the engine's from an own-keys lookup, else none. `PipelineMark` uses it with the mark's name as alt text; `EngineMark` keeps the engine's. `PipelineMark` replaces `EngineMark` in `RunState.tsx` (`Pipeline`, used by the runs list), `HomePage.tsx`, `RunHeader.tsx` and the Launch Loaded card. `web/src/assets/logos/logo-nf-core.png` is a byte copy of `docs/assets/logos/logo-nf-core.png` (same sha256, see Evidence).
- **Copy** (`eb46400`): `runs/CopyMenu.tsx` (99 lines, new) is a menu of native buttons with arrow, Home, End, Escape and Tab handling, focus returned to Copy, and a `role="status"` line for 4 s. `copyText` is shared with the older `CopyButton`. `RunHeader.tsx` gains `copyItems` (Config as `params.json` or `config.yaml` when the run has params, Run directory for a driven run, Run ID) and `runDirectory`, now also used by the Run directory fact.
- **The Logs tab** (`eb46400`, `4506ab5`):
    - `runSearch` validates `?tab=` and, on Logs only, `&source=`; `routes.tsx` wires it as the route's `validateSearch`. The page reads the tab and source from the URL and replaces the history entry on a change. A `lastSource` ref keeps the source picked last, so Logs reopens on it after another tab (CR-P3PARC-02, `4506ab5`).
    - `logFile` builds the file's path from `runs_root`, the run ID and the launch layout: `.spectio/head.out`, `.spectio/head.err`, `.nextflow.log`, or Snakemake's `.snakemake/log/` with "the newest .log there". None for a run from a terminal. The File row has a Copy button.
    - The viewer (`sp-nowrap`) scrolls sideways, is focusable, and scrolls to its end when its text loads. `level()` adds `ok` ("completed", "Finished job", "(100%) done") and `progress` ("progress N"); `theme.ts` adds the two colours `code-ok` and `code-progress`.
    - Open raw is a link to `logUrl(id, source)`, the same `GET /runs/{id}/logs`, in a new tab with `rel="noopener noreferrer"`.
    - The note under the viewer ends with "open a task in Tasks" (or "a job in Jobs"), which opens the Tasks tab. The Summary's log links focus the Logs panel once it shows.
    - The log tail in the aside does not wrap either.
- **Boards** (`eb46400`): LiteLaunch and LiteLaunchSnakemake draw one parameter per line (`.plist`, `.prow`, `.pmeta`), the meta moved out of the help.
- **The smoke** (`eb46400`): `tests/e2e/skeleton.spec.ts` clicks the first Launch button, since a desktop now has two.
- **The inbox** (`eb46400`, then the docs commit): #91 gets Thomas's answer (b), the logo checksums, and the provenance kept open; the docs commit marks rule 1 and the Assumed line superseded and says the name is a claim (CR-P3PARC-07, SEC-P3PARC-02).
- **Review fixes:** `ae60a73` (round 1), `4506ab5` (round 2), `089adc3` (the simplifier). The [review record](../../reviews/code/P3-parC.md) lists every finding with its commit.
- **Tests:** 26 new web tests, so 140 in 15 files (114 in 14 after UI parity B). `shell/EngineMark.test.tsx` (5) is new; `RunDetailPage.test.tsx` +12, `LaunchPage.test.tsx` +8, `icons.test.ts` +1; one test of `RunsPage.test.tsx` is rewritten for the nf-core mark, and `HomePage.test.tsx` gains mark checks inside an existing test.
- **Specs**, by the docs commit; no section number changed: ui-spec notes "UI parity C" in §4.3 (decided, #91 (b)), §4.4 and §4.6 (*assumed — standing rule (unattended mode)*). The 2026-10-31 "Marks in 0.0.1" bullet of §4.3 is marked superseded.
- **Decision log:** [2026-10-31 — UI parity C](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-31-ui-parity-c-parameters-one-per-line-the-logos-the-runs-copy-menu-and-logs-written-into-ui-spec-43-44-and-46-p3-parity-c-inbox-91).
- **Docs of this step:** this section, the review record, the ui-spec notes, the decision-log entry, the #91 edits, and the `p3-parc-launch-logs` figure (`tools/diagrams/p3_parc_launch_logs.py`, by `diagram-artist`, `670edd5`) with its manifest entry.

#### Differences from the request and the boards (UI parity C)

| Board or request | Code | Why |
|---|---|---|
| LiteRunActions: "Parameters (params.json)" | "Config (params.json)", and "Config (config.yaml)" for Snakemake | one word for both engines; recorded in the ui-spec §4.4 note (CR-P3PARC-03 r2); the board follows at the next `/apply-findings` |
| LiteRunActions: a terminal run copies Run directory and Run ID | a terminal run also gets Config when the API serves its params; no Run directory, since the API does not serve it | the same note |
| LiteRunActions: a Command item | not built | the API does not serve the engine's command line yet (P1 Task 32) |
| LiteLaunch: a field's error under its input | the error renders after the help | Mantine places `error` after the `inputContainer` (CR-P3PARC-04 r2, open: FU-P3-27) |
| LiteRunLogs: the file's path, from the server | built by the screen from `runs_root` and the launch layout; Snakemake's names a directory | `/logs` returns text only; the UI parity A note had left the path to P1 Task 32 |
| The 2026-10-30 note: no Launch in the page head (UX-227) | Launch in the head and under the aside on a desktop | as LiteLaunch; the open point of UI parity B is closed by the ui-spec §4.6 UI parity C note |

### Public demo (P3-demo)

Branch `autopilot/P3-demo`, base `4c4e3ba` (UI parity C on `main`, promotion #110), code head `8244642`, figure `6d92a7d`, then this page. From the base to `8244642` there are 4 commits: 21 files, +934 −16. Of these, `web/` is +707 −15 in 15 files (`web/demo/fixtures.json` alone is +207: 41 answers on 207 lines, 773 205 bytes); `tools/media/` is +203 −1 in 3 files; the two workflow files and `.gitignore` are +24 in 3 files. There is no server change (`git diff 4c4e3ba 8244642 -- packages` is empty) and no new npm or Python dependency. The workflow gains one pinned action, `actions/setup-node` at `49933ea` (v4.4.0).

Ownership: how the demo is built and published is the [dev-env §7.3 note of 2026-10-31](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#73-the-ui-in-mock-mode-from-the-first-ui-milestone); the recipe to record it again is in [`tools/media/README.md`](https://github.com/spectio-org/spectio/blob/main/tools/media/README.md), "The public demo". The open security points before it is published are [inbox #92](../../reviews/inbox.md#92-assumed-what-the-public-demo-leaves-open-before-it-is-published).

**What the brief asked, and where it is:**

| Brief item (2026-10-31) | What the step did | Where |
|---|---|---|
| a demo mode chosen at build time only | `npm --prefix web run build:demo` is `vite --mode demo`: `web/dist-demo`, `base: "./"`. `main.tsx` imports `./demo` only in that mode, so the normal build has neither the code nor the recording | `vite.config.ts`, `package.json`, `main.tsx`, `demo/index.ts` |
| the normal build holds no demo | `npm run build` ends with `check-dist.mjs`, which fails on any recorded run ID or the texts "Demo: recorded data", "Read-only demo" and "read-only-demo" in `web/dist` | `web/scripts/check-dist.mjs` |
| every `GET` from the recording; filtered lists; unknown path `404` | exact path and query (parameters sorted); `/runs` filtered by `state`, `q` (a name prefix) and `sort=-ended_at`, `/runs/{id}/tasks` by `state` and `process`, both paged by `limit` and an offset cursor; anything else `404` in the problem shape | `demo/stub.ts` `answer`, `LISTS`, `listPage` |
| writes refused with `403` and the message | `POST`, `PUT`, `PATCH`, `DELETE`: `403`, slug `read-only-demo`, "Read-only demo: launching and cancelling are disabled" | `stub.ts` `READ_ONLY` |
| the Launch form loads, Launch shows the reason through the existing mechanism | `reason()` returns `READ_ONLY` in demo mode, so the button is `aria-disabled` and described by it, as for any other reason | `launch/LaunchPage.tsx`, one line |
| the event stream opens and stays quiet | `QuietEventSource`: opens once, never errors, never reconnects | `stub.ts` |
| a banner on every page with a docs link | an `<aside aria-label="About this demo">` above the router, in the review tint, with a link to `https://spectio.pages.dev/` | `demo/DemoBanner.tsx`, `demo/demo.css` |
| deep links and reload on a static host | hash history in demo mode (`#/runs/…`) | `routes.tsx` |
| Open raw and Download work or are disabled | Download already works from the shown text; Open raw becomes a `blob:` URL of that text, disabled until it loads | `runs/RunDetailPage.tsx` |
| times that stay true and do not lie | every ISO time in a JSON answer moves by one offset, so that the newest run ended one hour before the page opened; logs keep the recorded times; the banner says both | `stub.ts` `shiftTimes`, `DemoBanner.tsx` |
| `nf-core/demo` marked as a demo copy | the banner: "a renamed copy of the synthetic test pipeline, not an nf-core pipeline"; the README recipe says the same | `DemoBanner.tsx`, `tools/media/README.md` |
| a recorder with the hygiene check and a size budget | `record_demo.py`: every `GET`, lists stored whole; Nextflow's `System: Linux <release>` cut; refuses on `sanitize.leaks`, its own host name or kernel release, or above 1.5 MB | `tools/media/record_demo.py`, `test_record_demo.py` |
| publishing for `main` and PR previews | after the MkDocs build: Node from `dev/versions.env`, `npm ci --ignore-scripts`, `build:demo`, `cp -R web/dist-demo site/demo`; the deploy check also fetches `$URL/demo/` | `.github/workflows/previews.yml`, `.github/zizmor.yml` |
| "Try the demo" in the nav and the README | `mkdocs.yml` nav links `/demo/` as an absolute path (no docs page under `demo/`, CR-P3DEMO-01); `README.md` links the production URL | the docs commit |

This list names the files.

- **The stub** (`f769837`): `web/src/demo/stub.ts` (139 lines, new). `install()` replaces `window.fetch` and `window.EventSource` and returns what puts them back. A request to another origin gets a `404` and never reaches the real `fetch`. The `/workflows:inspect` of a pipeline that was not recorded answers `422` on `location`, naming the three recorded pipelines (CR-P3DEMO-03 r1, kept: see the dev-env note).
- **The entry** (`f769837`): `web/src/demo/index.ts` imports the recording as a raw string (`?raw`), shifts its times against `new Date()` and installs the stub before the first render.
- **The banner** (`f769837`, `06b1117`, `25aa0c9`, `8244642`): one plain span with the whole disclosure, then the docs link (`white-space: nowrap`). A layout effect measures the aside and sets `--sp-demo-banner-h` on the root element with `style.setProperty` (CSSOM, so a CSP needs no inline style); a `ResizeObserver` measures it again on resize; the cleanup removes the property. `shell/shell.css` `.sp-sidebar` takes `var(--sp-demo-banner-h, 0px)` off its height, so the normal build is unchanged (CR-P3DEMO-02 r1, CR-P3DEMO-03 r2, CR-P3DEMO-05 r3).
- **The recording** (`f769837`, `06b1117`): `web/demo/fixtures.json`, 41 answers: `/version`, `/runs:summary?group_by=state`, `/runs`, five runs with their run, processes, stages, tasks and three logs each, and three inspections. The five runs: two plain runs of the synthetic Nextflow copy, one failed in `MERGE`, `variant-calling-batch-3`, `cohort-2026-scale` (2069 tasks) and `nf-core/demo-52FEZ5` from the demo copy `/g/demo-lab/pipelines/nf-core-demo`. `06b1117` rewrote the four `System: Linux <release>` lines (SEC-P3DEMO-02 r1).
- **The publishing** (`f769837`): the three steps of `previews.yml` above. `.github/zizmor.yml` ignores `cache-poisoning` at `previews.yml:110`, because setup-node v4.4.0 caches only when its `cache` input is set, and it is not.
- **Tests:** 18 new web tests in 2 files, so 158 in 17 (140 in 15 after UI parity C): `demo/stub.test.ts` (13) and `demo/demo.test.tsx` (5); 3 Python tests in `tools/media/test_record_demo.py`, run by hand (no CI job runs them: FU-P3-29).
- **Review fixes:** `06b1117` (round 1), `25aa0c9` (round 2), `8244642` (the simplifier). The [review record](../../reviews/code/P3-demo.md) lists every finding with its commit.
- **Specs**, by the docs commit; no section number changed: the [dev-env §7.3 note](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#73-the-ui-in-mock-mode-from-the-first-ui-milestone) (decided by Thomas for publishing; *assumed — standing rule* for how it is built).
- **Decision log:** [2026-10-31 — a public read-only demo](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-31-a-public-read-only-demo-of-the-001-screens-at-demo-written-into-dev-env-73-p3-demo).
- **Docs of this step:** this section, the review record, the dev-env note, the decision-log entry, [inbox #92](../../reviews/inbox.md#92-assumed-what-the-public-demo-leaves-open-before-it-is-published), the `tools/media/README.md` corrections (the byte count, the kernel guard, the shot recipe), the "Try the demo" links in `mkdocs.yml` and `README.md`, the `demo-runs` shot (one `Shot` entry in `tools/media/shots.py`, one manifest entry), and the `p3-demo-static-demo` figure (`tools/diagrams/p3_demo_static_demo.py`, by `diagram-artist`, `6d92a7d`) with its manifest entry.

#### Differences from the brief and the spec (P3-demo)

| Brief or spec | Code | Why |
|---|---|---|
| dev-env §7.3: fixtures generated from the OpenAPI and the demo dataset, served by MSW | answers recorded from a demo server, served by a 139-line `fetch` stub | the brief's choice: real answers of the real server, no new dependency; recorded in the §7.3 note |
| the brief: an unknown path answers `404` | an unrecorded pipeline's inspect answers `422`, naming the recorded ones | it tells a visitor what works (CR-P3DEMO-03 r1); recorded in the §7.3 note *(assumed)* |
| the brief: the hygiene check allows only alice, login1 and `/g/demo-lab` | the `fixtures.yaml` denylist, the host name and the kernel release | no allowlist exists in `tools/media/` (SEC-P3DEMO-01 r1, open: FU-P3-29) |
| the brief: ruff on the new script | `ruff` reports 4 errors on the recorder and its test | `tools/` is outside ruff's `include`, so the gate does not run it (CR-P3DEMO-04 r3, open: FU-P3-29) |
| the screens' subtitle: "live, updated N s ago" | the same words in the demo, over a stream that never sends | the screens are not changed for the demo beyond the three places above; the banner says the data is recorded |

### UI parity D · Run detail sheets, searchable logs, test pipelines in Launch

Branch `autopilot/P3-parD`, base `5ce1f89` (after UI parity C, #109, and its promotion #110; P3-demo's PR #111 open, not merged), code head `846f462`, then the figure and this page. From the base to `846f462` there are 3 commits: 23 files, +1756 −128. Of these, `web/` is +1545 −127 in 20 files; the two schemas are +205 in 2 new files; `test_launch.py` is +6 −1. There is no server or API change (`git diff 5ce1f89 846f462 -- packages/*/src` is empty) and no new dependency.

Ownership:

- the screens: the ui-spec notes "UI parity D" of 2026-10-31 in [§4.4](../../specs/ui-spec.md#44-run-detail) (the sheets, the URL's process, the chips, First to fail, Copy, the log search) and [§4.6](../../specs/ui-spec.md#46-launch-and-relaunch) (Try a test pipeline, `/launch?location=`);
- the schemas: the 2026-10-31 note of [dev-env §10.1](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#101-synthetic-scenario-pipelines-testspipelinessynthetic);
- what the API serves: [api §5](../../specs/api-and-data-model.md) is unchanged; per-task files, exit code and resources stay with P1 Tasks 28, 29 and 32.

**Thomas's points:**

| Point (2026-10-31) | What the step did | Where |
|---|---|---|
| "steps & stages pas clickable, pas de détails" | every stage, process (rule) and task (job) opens its detail in one modal side sheet; Open in Tasks; `?tab=tasks&process=` | `DetailSheet.tsx` (new), `RunDetailPage.tsx` (`OpenProcess`, `OpenTask`, "Stage details", `runSearch`) |
| "plein de parties et d'éléments pas encore clickable" | the inventory below: four task chips filter the Tasks tab, Copy on the pipeline directory, First to fail opens its process, Home's pipelines open Launch | `RunHeader.tsx`, `RunDetailPage.tsx` (`WhyItFailed`), `HomePage.tsx`, `routes.tsx` |
| "params quasi vides pour les wf" | parameter schemas for both synthetic pipelines, four groups each | `tests/pipelines/synthetic/nextflow/nextflow_schema.json`, `.../snakemake/workflow/schemas/config.schema.yaml` (new); `icons.tsx` (two `fa_icon` names) |
| "pas de wf par défaut de test dans launch pour tester" | "Try a test pipeline": the server's recent pipelines, one click fills and loads | `TryPipelines.tsx` (new), `LaunchPage.tsx` (`pick`, `load(at, rev)`) |
| "logs pas searchable" | search, an "n / m" counter, previous and next, Enter, Shift+Enter, Escape, and a level filter in the Logs tab's viewer | `LogViewer.tsx` (new) |
| "pas de logs et info/outputs/err/out par process et task" | not in this step: the API does not serve them. The task detail says so and names the tasks that bring them | P1 Tasks 28, 29 and 32 |

This list names the files.

- **The detail sheet** (`50ff24c`, `2a45be9`): `runs/DetailSheet.tsx` (265 lines, new) is a Mantine `Drawer` at the right, 560 px wide, full width on the phone, with `returnFocus` and a Close button. Its views are a stack (`views`, the shown one last) with "Back to <previous>"; a new view takes the focus.
    - `ProcessView`: the full name, the state word, the stage line, `CountChips` (done of all, running, pending, failed, cached, retried), Open in Tasks, and `ProcessTasks`, which pages `listTasks(id, {process, limit: 50, cursor})` with Load more. A `loading` state guards Load more (CR-P3PARD-06, `2a45be9`).
    - `StageView`: position, Declared or Detected, state, counts, and its processes as buttons.
    - `TaskView`: ID, Name, Tag, State, Process (a button back to it) and Stage, then the `role="note"` of the §4.4 note, worded for each engine.
- **The run page** (`50ff24c`, `2a45be9`): `RunDetailPage.tsx` gains `OpenProcess` and `OpenTask` (native buttons whose label names the row), "Stage details" in each open stage, and the sheet's state (`views`). `runSearch` keeps `process` on the Tasks tab, and `TasksPanel` takes its process from the URL. `focusTasks` focuses the Tasks panel when the view is already shown (CR-P3PARD-01). `WhyItFailed` looks up `failure.task_id` among the failed tasks, up to 5 pages of 50 (CR-P3PARD-05), and shows "First to fail" with a button to its process. `ProcessName`, `taskLabel`, `total`, `lastSegment` and `CopyButton` moved to `RunState.tsx`, `stateWord.ts` and `CopyMenu.tsx`, shared with the sheet.
- **The header** (`50ff24c`): `taskChips` gives Succeeded, Running, Pending and Failed a `filter`; those chips are buttons (`onChip`). The Pipeline directory fact has a `CopyButton`.
- **The log viewer** (`50ff24c`, `2a45be9`, `846f462`): `runs/LogViewer.tsx` (178 lines, new) holds `level()` and `LogLines`, moved from `RunDetailPage.tsx`, and `LogViewer`: a labelled search field, the query applied 150 ms after the last key, matches found by an escaped case-insensitive `RegExp` and drawn as `<mark>`, the current one with `data-current` and scrolled into view; the counter in `aria-live="polite"`; Previous and Next; Enter, Shift+Enter, Escape; the All, INFO, WARN and ERROR buttons with counts. Each line is a memoised `Line`. `splitLines` drops a final newline (CR-P3PARD-04). The simplifier gave `Line` one render path (`846f462`).
- **Launch and Home** (`50ff24c`): `launch/TryPipelines.tsx` (63 lines, new): `recentPipelines` keeps the distinct locations of `GET /runs?limit=50`, at most five; `useRecentRuns` reads them once; `TryPipelines` draws the buttons, or the docs link when there is none. `LaunchPage.tsx` gets `recent` and `initialLocation`; `pick()` fills the field, loads a directory and focuses Revision for a repository. `routes.tsx` adds `launchSearch` (`?location=`). Home's "Your pipelines" rows are links to `/launch?location=`.
- **CSS** (`50ff24c`): `runs.css` +137 (the sheet, its rows, the chip buttons, the search tools, the marks), `app.css` +59 (Try a test pipeline).
- **The schemas** (`50ff24c`): see the [dev-env §10.1 note](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#101-synthetic-scenario-pipelines-testspipelinessynthetic). `test_launch.py`'s `test_inspect_detects_engines` now expects `schema_source` and the four group titles for both engines; the "no schema" case stays covered by other tests of that file.
- **The demo server** (not in the repository): the worker copied both schemas into the demo copies under `/g/demo-lab/pipelines/` (`synthetic`, `nf-core-demo`, which keeps `manifest.name` `nf-core/demo`, and `synthetic-smk`), the built screens into the demo's `web/dist`, and launched one Nextflow run with five values set (Evidence). No Snakemake run: Snakemake is not installed in the demo's virtual environment.
- **Review fixes:** `2a45be9` (round 1), `846f462` (the simplifier). The [review record](../../reviews/code/P3-parD.md) lists every finding with its commit.
- **Tests:** 35 new web tests, so 175 in 18 files (140 in 15 after UI parity C): `DetailSheet.test.tsx` (10), `LogViewer.test.tsx` (11) and `TryPipelines.test.tsx` (6) are new; `RunDetailPage.test.tsx` +6, `HomePage.test.tsx` +1, `icons.test.ts` +1.
- **Specs**, by the docs commit; no section number changed: ui-spec notes "UI parity D" in [§4.2](../../specs/ui-spec.md#42-overview) (Home's "Your pipelines" links, added after doc review), §4.4 and §4.6, and a dev-env §10.1 note, all *assumed — standing rule (unattended mode)*.
- **Decision log:** [2026-10-31 — UI parity D](../../decisions/log.md#2026-10-31-ui-parity-d-run-detail-sheets-searchable-logs-test-pipelines-in-launch-written-into-ui-spec-44-and-46-and-dev-env-101-p3-parity-d).
- **Docs of this step:** this section, the review record, the four spec notes, the decision-log entry, and the `p3-pard-sheets-search` figure (`tools/diagrams/p3_pard_sheets_search.py`, by `diagram-artist`).
- **Media** (the docs fix after review): `tools/media/shots.py` gains `app-run-process`; `app-launch` now picks its pipeline from Try a test pipeline; `app-run-detail` and `app-run-detail-failed` get dark twins; `app-run-logs` searches for MERGE. `tools/media/live.js`'s `fill:` reaches a search field too (one line). `docs/assets/media.yaml` and `tools/media/README.md` follow. These tooling lines (`24f7cae3`) did not go through `review-code`; their review is an item of follow-up FU-P3-28.

|---|---|---|
| Search "in every log/tail viewer" | the Logs tab only; the Summary's six-line tails link to it | a six-line tail has little to search (CR-P3PARD-03); recorded in the ui-spec §4.4 note |
| "pipeline names on Home/rows → Launch prefilled" | Home's "Your pipelines" rows only; the runs list's Workflow column stays text | the column is not styled as a link, so it does not look interactive |
| "Launch prefilled" from a link | a directory from `/launch?location=` is filled and loaded at once | the step's choice, the same rule as a Try click; SEC-P3PARD-01 (open, FU-P3-28) recommends filling only |
| LiteTaskFiles, MLiteTaskFilesSheet: the job's files, exit code, attempts, resources | the task sheet shows ID, name, tag, state, process and stage, and a note naming P1 Tasks 28 and 29 | the 0.0.1 API does not serve them; no fake data and no dead button |
| Two new runs on the demo, Nextflow and Snakemake | one Nextflow run | Snakemake is not installed in `/opt/spectio-demo-venv` |
| Retake `app-launch`, `app-launch-snakemake`, `app-run-detail`, `app-run-detail-failed`, `app-run-logs`; new `app-run-process` and a log-search shot | taken on `846f462`, light and dark: `app-launch`, `app-run-detail`, `app-run-detail-failed`, `app-run-logs` (now with a search) and the new `app-run-process`; `app-launch-snakemake` not retaken | its recipe `APP_LAUNCH_SMK` waits for a `Parameters defaults` button that the new schema's groups replaced; FU-P3-28 (Figures and media) |
| LiteRunDetail: "Stage details" | not in a live shot | the demo's pipelines declare no stage and none is detected ("No stages for this pipeline"); the step's test covers it |
| Re-record #111's `web/demo/fixtures.json` | not done | the file is not on this branch; the step after both PRs merge re-records it (FU-P3-28) |

**A real `spectio serve`,** by `doc-writer` on `3cbf735`, from the worktree's `.venv`, in a fresh 0700 demo home (shown as `~`), node `vm`, uid 0. No `nextflow` or `snakemake` is installed here. The pipelines are the repository's synthetic ones; their path is shown as `<synthetic>`. The script is `api-demo.sh`, in the session's scratch directory. The transcript below is **not verbatim**:

- The commands are simplified. The script runs them with shell variables, waits for the port before the first request, filters out the `spectio init` lines that mention `nf-spectio`, and reads the index with a short Python script.
- The two inspect answers went through Python's `json.dumps`, which adds a space after each `:` and `,`; the server sends none. The Snakemake answer keeps four keys only. The other answers are as the server sent them.
- The server's log lines are left out. `spectio serve` prints alembic `INFO` lines before the start text, and uvicorn startup and access-log lines after it. The transcript keeps the three start-text lines only.
- The script sets `TMPDIR=~/tmp`, so the database is in `~/tmp/spectio-0`. Without `TMPDIR` it is `/tmp/spectio-0` (the default `$TMPDIR/spectio-<uid>`, else `/tmp/spectio-<uid>`, of `spectio_server/store/db.py`).

```console
$ spectio init --local --port 18081

warning: snakemake not found on the login shell's PATH: give it with --snakemake

$ kill -TERM <pid of serve>; wait
serve exit 0
```

Four things to read from it:

- This machine has no other person's process (`/proc` shows uids 0 and 64321; 64321 has no account), no `SLURM_CONF`, no `slurm.conf` and no `sinfo`, so `shared_host()` returns `None` and the API is mounted.
- Every refusal is a problem document: `422` names its field, the browser checks answer `400`, `403` and `415`.
- The `201` writes the run directory and its manifest, and the runner leases the `launch` command at once. With no `nextflow` here, the launch fails `engine_missing`, as Task 9 defines; the run and the command both end `failed`.
- The SIGTERM stop exits 0.


The 15 deselected are the `engines` tests, as after Task 11. The session runs as uid 0, so the root-only tests ran.

**Test-first,** from the worker's report: the round-5 fixes were written as tests first, and `test_serve_decides_the_api_once`, `test_inspect_symlinked_directory_not_followed` (extended) and `test_inspect_symlinked_profile_not_listed` failed before the change. The simplifier's two commits (−13 +16) kept the gate at 429 passed ([record](../../reviews/code/P3-t17.md)).

**Review** ([record](../../reviews/code/P3-t17.md)). Finding IDs repeat across rounds, so each carries its round:

- Round 1, on `4aaff5b`: `code-reviewer` **changes**, `security-reviewer` **block**. The blockers, CR-P3T17-01 and SEC-P3T17-01 (r1): no Host allow-list and no Origin check, so DNS rebinding lets any web page launch a pipeline as the user. Majors: a cross-site `GET` of inspect starts a clone (SEC-P3T17-02), a quadratic `nextflow.config` tokenizer (SEC-P3T17-03), remote `$ref`s fetched (SEC-P3T17-04), and the copied rebuild code (CR-P3T17-02). Fixed in `d9dcc6d`, except the clone size cap.
- Round 2, on `d9dcc6d`: `code-reviewer` **changes**: the tokenizer was still quadratic in two cases (CR-P3T17-03 (r2)), fixed in `a837e46`. `security-reviewer` **approve** with 3 minors.
- Round 3, on `a837e46`: `code-reviewer` **approve**. `security-reviewer` **changes**: inspect followed symlinks out of a clone (SEC-P3T17-05 (r3)), and kind `local` set on a login node served the session-less API to every user (SEC-P3T17-06 (r3)). Both fixed in `2d64827`.
- Round 4, on `2d64827`: both **approve**. CR-P3T17-04 (the shared-host decision taken twice) and SEC-P3T17-08 (symlinks in `profiles` and entry files) fixed in `5e4d12f`.
- Round 5, on `5e4d12f`: both **approve**, 2 minors and 2 simplify.
- `code-simplifier`, after round 5: `d2d4223` and `3cbf735` (CR-P3T17-05 and -06 (r5)).
- Open: see [Findings and open questions](#findings-and-open-questions).

```text
CODE  step=P3-t17  review=approve b/M/m=1/2/13  security=approve b/M/m=1/5/14  rounds=5  simplify=changed -13/+16  tests=pass
```

The counts add up the five rounds per reviewer; CR-P3T17-02 (r1), "major simplify", counts as a major, and the three plain `simplify` findings are not counted.

**A real `spectio serve`,** by `doc-writer` on `562510b`, from the worktree's `.venv`, in a fresh 0700 demo home (shown as `~`), node `vm`, uid 0. No `nextflow`, `snakemake` or built bridge is installed here, so a launch fails at once with `engine_missing` and a live `202` cancel cannot be shown; `test_cancel_reaches_the_runner` covers that path. The pipeline is the repository's synthetic Nextflow one, shown as `<synthetic>/nextflow`. The script is `read-demo.sh`, in the session's scratch directory. The transcript below is **not verbatim**:

- The commands are simplified: the script uses shell variables, waits for the port, and reads the run ID from the `201`. The `POST /runs` body is the one of the Task 17 transcript, with `name: demo-run`.
- The server's log lines and the `spectio init` lines about `nf-spectio` are left out. The `405` comes from a second, identical session (port 18083), because the first `PUT` was sent without `Origin` and got the write check's `403` first.
- The answers are as the server sent them, the path replaced.


Five things to read from it:

- `workflow.version` is `0.1.0`, the `manifest.version` inspect read at launch; `engine_version` is `null` because no head started; `head` is `null` for the same reason (#87 rules 4 and 6).
- The stream sent a frame for the new run (`seq_through: 0`, the CR-P3T18-01 fix), then one for its failure.
- `:cancel` on a run that already ended answers `200`; without `Origin` it is the write check's `403`.
- Every refusal is a problem document, the body nested 500 000 levels deep and the wrong method included.
- The `405` says `allow: POST`, although `GET /runs` is served too. Starlette hands a method mismatch to the first route whose path matches and takes `Allow` from that route alone (`starlette/routing.py:281-282` and `:706-707` in the worktree's `.venv`, Starlette 1.10.0, read 2026-10-30). The `POST /runs` router is mounted first. This is an observation of this session, not a review finding: follow-up FU-P3-20.

**SEC-P3T20-02 (r2), test-first,** re-checked by `doc-writer`. The new test passes on `4a70188`; run against the `heads()` of `e6dc116` (before the fix, through the test module with `heads_function` swapped), it fails, and that `heads()` names PID 1 for a forged line:

```console
$ uv run --locked pytest packages/spectio/tests/test_serve_local.py -v
…::test_heads_names_a_live_group_leader_and_refuses_forgeries PASSED
…::test_heads_refuses_a_pid_that_does_not_lead_its_group PASSED

test_heads_refuses_a_pid_that_does_not_lead_its_group FAILED
old heads() on a forged '1 1 <start of PID 1> <boot> host' line names: ['1']
$ bash -n tests/e2e/serve-local.sh && echo "bash -n ok"
bash -n ok
```

`stop()` runs `kill -TERM -- "-$pid"` for each name, so the old code would have signalled every process of the user. `shellcheck` is not installed in this session and was not run.

The first test checks All runs' `href="/runs"` (CR-P3PARA-02), the second checks `q` (CR-P3PARA-01), the next two check the tail (CR-P3PARA-04), and the last checks Download (CR-P3PARA-08). On `7a4811c` all five pass, as shown above. The two tail tests check the `data-tone` attribute, not a colour. In the built CSS, only `.sp-log span[data-tone="failed"]` and `[data-tone="review"]` set one (`runs.css:750-755`), and the tail is a `pre.sp-code.sp-log-tail`. So at `7a4811c` the tail's lines stayed untinted. CR-P3PARA-04 is fixed in `36a7af2`: the tail tint was scoped to `.sp-log` only, and the rule now covers `.sp-log-tail` too (no test reads the colour, which jsdom cannot give).

**Compared with the boards.** `doc-writer` looked at the eight live shots below. What the step fixed shows: the phone title rows and Back to Runs, the cards, the two-by-two count cards, the pinned bar's summary above a full-width Launch, the folding Snakemake sections with dotted keys, the YAML preview with the whole `ref` map, the Loaded card and "Where it runs". The differences left are the open items under [Findings and open questions](#findings-and-open-questions): the field hints above the input, Launch under the aside, no "ACTIVE · n" group, and the leading "·" when the phone header's line wraps (visible in `app-run-detail-phone`: "· ended Today 11:07" starts a line).

**Review** ([record](../../reviews/code/P3-parB.md)). Rounds 1 to 3 ran in the `review-code` workflow, which stopped after two fix rounds without approval; round 4 was run by hand on `8c86467..614b81f`, and round 5 on the three fix commits after it (`dcec010`, `db620dc`, `b092b7c`):

| Round | Head | code-reviewer | security-reviewer |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `35ed465` | **approve** (5 minors, 1 simplify) | **changes** (1 major: SEC-P3PARB-01, 1 minor) |
| 2 | `8c86467` | **approve** (5 minors, 1 simplify) | **approve** (1 minor; SEC-P3PARB-01 recorded as fixed) |
| 3 | `8c86467` | **changes** (1 major: CR-P3PARB-01, 5 minors, 2 simplify) | **approve** (2 minors) |
| 4 | `8c86467..614b81f` | **changes** (1 major: CR-P3PARB-R4-01, already fixed in `dcec010`; 3 minors, two fixed in `db620dc`, R4-03 not fixed) | **approve** (none) |
| 5 | `dcec010`, `db620dc`, `b092b7c` | **approve** (1 minor and 1 simplify, relayed as one finding: CR-P3PARB-R5-01, fixed in `e6de280`) | not run; its last verdict is round 4's approve |

```text
CODE  step=P3-parB  review=approve b/M/m=0/2/19  security=approve b/M/m=0/1/4  rounds=5  simplify=not-run -0/+0  tests=pass
```

The counts add up the five rounds of `code-reviewer` and the four of `security-reviewer`, from each report's own count line; the five `simplify` findings are not counted. `review=approve` is round 5's verdict. Round 4's was `changes`, for a major already fixed in `dcec010`. `code-simplifier` did not run: its findings were fixed by hand in `614b81f` and `e6de280`, except CR-P3PARB-08. Round 5 found that `db620dc`'s answer to CR-P3PARB-R4-03 never changed behaviour: `engine` is null only before a load or when no engine is guessed, so `engine ?? guess` always equals `engine`. `e6de280` drops it, and R4-03 goes whole to FU-P3-26. `e6de280` itself has had no review (+3 −4, a variable removed). Security did not run in round 5.

**Exit check.** Every finding of the step is fixed or moved with an owner (the table under What changed, and [Findings and open questions](#findings-and-open-questions)). The 114 web tests, `tsc`, the build, the default gate and the smoke pass, and the live shots match the boards by eye, with the differences listed above.

```console
$ curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8766/api/v1/runs/run_01M69K5J5WV07JC34DA6Q6GTTV/processes
ALIGN {'succeeded': 144} 144
…   # the last number is the retries; MAKE_READS, QC_STATS and SPLIT 24 each, QC_REPORT, MERGE and FINAL_REPORT 1 each, COMPARE 2, each with 0
```

The output is from the worker's transcript; `...` and `…` mark cuts. The run's params hold the five values sent, which its Parameters tab lists. The demo server serves the bundle `index-Dp2VnTMK.js` of `50ff24c`, not the fixes of `2a45be9`.

**The live shots**, by `doc-writer` after the review, on the same demo server (`alice`, node `login1`). `app-launch-snakemake`'s recipe was tried and stops: `waiting for getByRole('button', { name: /^Parameters defaults/ })`, `Timeout 30000ms exceeded`. With the shared `APP_LAUNCH` steps changed to Try a test pipeline, `app-launch-phone`'s recipe runs (`ok`); its files were not retaken.

`code-simplifier` made one change after round 2, `846f462`: `LogViewer`'s `Line` has one render path. Its checks gave the same results before and after: pytest 511 passed, `tsc` clean, vitest 175 of 175.

**Exit check.** Each of Thomas's points is answered within the 0.0.1 API, or sent with its reason to P1 Tasks 28, 29 and 32 (the table under What changed); the inventory covers every screen of the brief; the round 1 fixes were written test-first; the web tests, `tsc`, the build and the default gate pass; the smoke passes without engines. The live shots were taken on `846f462`, light and dark (Figures and media, and the commands below).

## Findings and open questions

- Minors left open:
    - the phone placeholders show no words;
    - folding is drawn by a prop, and G4 makes it work (done in G4);
    - the theme switch has no dark palette yet (done in G4);
    - UX-G1-09 waits for the next `/apply-findings`. It covers the ui-spec §2 note for #72 (b) and the phone theme switch, and the 2026-10-28 phone note (ui-spec §2), which still says the first row holds only the logo and the login node.
- The [Progress index](../index.md) cannot link the P3-M* pages yet: `hooks/progress.py` `_table` looks only for `W5/P3.md`. This is follow-up FU-P3-01, already open. This page is not in the `mkdocs.yml` nav either: it is reached only from the dev-env §15.3 notes, the decision log and P2. FU-P3-01 stays open until P3-M4 closes. When it closes, check that this page is in the `mkdocs.yml` nav and in the Progress index: today the build reports `progress/W5/P3-M4.md` as not included in the nav.
- UX-G1-12 (below 1000 px the folded crumbs, including the link back to the run, cannot be reached) and UX-G1-13 (phone placeholders 40 px wide, not 44) are fixed in G3a.
- G2 open items ([record](../../reviews/code/P3-G2.md)):
    - to the next `/apply-findings`: UX-G2-04 (major: `GET /workflows:inspect` does not return the engine files it found; it must land before Task 17), UX-G2-05 (the ui-spec §4.6 wording), UX-G2-06 (the mvp-0.0.1 §2 note and alt text) and UX-G2-14 (G5's tests on `GET /workflows:inspect`);
    - to Task 19: UX-G2-07 (two toggle buttons for a one-of-two choice), UX-G2-08 (the disabled Revision field takes no focus), UX-G2-17 (the sticky aside on a short screen) and whether an edit cancels a fetch.
- G3a items left open ([`reviews/code/P3-G3a.md`](../../reviews/code/P3-G3a.md)):
    - for the next `/apply-findings`:
        - the tablet range of ui-spec §4.11 (768–1199 px, folded sidebar) is not drawn on the desktop boards; the shell code follows in Task 19 and G4;
        - a tint rule for page-head tiles: `-100` on the grey phone page, `-50` on white;
        - UX-G3a-14, a ui-spec §4.11 note: on the phone, Home shows its greeting as a body page head and the top bar carries no title; every other top-level page keeps the title and subtitle in the compact top bar;
    - for G4: the runs table scrolls sideways at 1024 px (UX-G3a-05), and the phone run title and state line are cut with an ellipsis (UX-G3a-06). G4 has no runs table or run title yet, so it leaves CSS for them (inbox #89 rule 6) and both move to P1 Task 20;
    - for G3b: the top bar's global link colour (UX-G3a-07), and the "… / Tasks" crumb of `LiteTaskFilesStates`.

**Task 17** ([record](../../reviews/code/P3-t17.md)): every finding not fixed in the branch, with its owner.

| Id | What | Goes to |
|---|---|---|
| CR-P3T17-01, SEC-P3T17-01 (r1); SEC-P3T17-06 (r3) | Fixed, but the posture stays a reserved question: may a local-mode API with no session be served before Tasks 12 to 16 at all? | Thomas, [inbox #86](../../reviews/inbox.md#86-assumed-the-001-launch-api-served-in-local-mode-only-before-sign-in) (reserved) |
| SEC-P3T17-07 (r4) | The shared-host check is a snapshot at start: a user who logs in later finds the API with no sign-in and can `POST /runs` from `curl`. The uid threshold is 1000, not `UID_MIN` | Thomas, inbox #86 option (d); then P1 Task 12 |
| SEC-P3T17-07 (r2) | The cross-site refusal fails open for a `GET` when the browser sends no `Sec-Fetch-Site`, no `Origin` and no `Referer` | P1 Tasks 12 and 13 (the `SameSite=Strict` session); the clone size cap below |
| CR-P3T17-06 (r2) | `api/browser.py` holds the checks of Tasks 14 and 16 | P1 Tasks 14 and 16 take it over (inbox #86 rule 1) |
| SEC-P3T17-02 (r1), SEC-P3T17-06 (r1), SEC-P3T17-10 (r3), SEC-P3T17-09 (r4), CR-P3T17-04 (r1) | No clone size cap and one clone kept per revision; the whole environment reaches git; git-lfs smudge; a cache key without scheme, port or ssh user; an ssh user starting with `-`; a launch that can never be adopted is retried on every tick | follow-up FU-P3-18 |
| SEC-P3T17-07 (r1), CR-P3T17-04 (r2), SEC-P3T17-06 (r2), SEC-P3T17-07 to -09 (r3), SEC-P3T17-09 (r5), CR-P3T17-06 (r2) | Answers that are 500, not 422 or 403 (`ENAMETOOLONG`, a malformed `Referer`, `{"$ref": "#"}`, a non-string `$schema`, a read error after the open, with a descriptor leak if `fstat` fails); no cap on the body, location, params or params depth; a catastrophic `pattern`; credentials in the access log; Unicode format characters accepted; the `NO_API` text still names the Host checks as missing | follow-up FU-P3-17 |
| CR-P3T17-03 (r1) and its four repeats, CR-P3T17-06 (r1), CR-P3T17-05 (r4) | The `StarletteDeprecationWarning` in every API test run; `wf.run_git = fetch` set without `monkeypatch`; a symlink test that accepts 200 or 422 | follow-up FU-P3-19 |
| CR-P3T17-05 (r1) | The rebuild accepts a Git manifest with no revision | follow-up FU-P3-15 (rebuild hardening) |

**Open points from Task 17:**

1. **Built before sign-in** *(assumed — pending Thomas, [inbox #86](../../reviews/inbox.md#86-assumed-the-001-launch-api-served-in-local-mode-only-before-sign-in))*. The API is mounted only in local mode, on a machine that does not look like a cluster node or a shared one, as decided once at start. The browser checks of dev-env §15.3 req. 4 and 7 run on every request; the session of req. 2 and 3 waits for Tasks 12 to 16.
2. **A branch clone is kept.** A branch that moved is not fetched again in 0.0.1 (#86 rule 4, option (c)).
3. **`check_engine` runs on every inspect,** through the login shell, for both engines, with no cache (#86 rule 8). It can make Load slow on a busy login node **[to be validated]** on the EMBL nodes.
4. **`test_sigterm_stops_cleanly` was reported flaky** by `code-reviewer` in round 1. It passed in every run of this session; it goes to FU-P3-19 with the test-hygiene items.
5. **FU-P3-14 is not done.** It was due "at the next change of `loop.py`, P1 Task 17 at the latest" ([P3-M2](P3-M2.md#findings-and-open-questions)). Task 17 did not change `loop.py` (it changed `sink.py`), so the 60 s grace for Snakemake ends stays; it moves to P1 Task 18.
6. **For the next `/apply-findings` pass:** P1 Task 17's interface, in the table [Differences from the plan's interface (Task 17)](#differences-from-the-plans-interface-task-17).
7. **The squash message must keep `Release-As: 0.0.1`** ([dev-env §15.2](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#152-how-the-first-tag-is-made)); `4aaff5b` carries it.

**Task 18** ([record](../../reviews/code/P3-t18.md)): every finding not fixed in the branch, with its owner.

**Open points from UI parity B:**

1. **The Snakemake and Nextflow merge rules are not relied on** *(assumed — standing rule, unattended mode)*. A nested map with one value set is sent whole, and on Nextflow a map stays one JSON field. Whether Snakemake merges a `--configfile` map key by key into the workflow's own config, and whether Nextflow's `-params-file` merges a partial map, is **[to be validated]** against their docs or code; until then the form does not depend on either.
2. **"Ending" depends on the end grace.** The word shows while the run is terminal and its head's end is not reported, which is up to 60 s for a Nextflow run, longer if the server stops inside the grace (it is reported when the next server adopts the launch).

1. **The merge is Thomas's.** The PR changes `previews.yml` and publishes a recording, so it is not auto-merged (the brief; triage policy, reserved: publishing). Its preview is the first run of the new workflow steps.
2. **§7.3's MSW plan.** The dev-env note says what 0.0.1 built; whether a later UI milestone keeps the recording or moves to MSW with generated fixtures waits for the next `/apply-findings` pass.
3. **The demo says "live".** The runs list's subtitle reads "live, updated 0 s ago" over a stream that never sends; the banner says the data is recorded. Changing the screens for the demo is out of the brief.
4. **The squash message must keep `Release-As: 0.0.1`** ([dev-env §15.2](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#152-how-the-first-tag-is-made)).

**Open points from UI parity D:**

1. **The docs link of Try a test pipeline** points at `https://spectio.pages.dev/specs/dev-environment-and-testing/#101-synthetic-scenario-pipelines-testspipelinessynthetic`. The anchor exists in this site's build (Evidence is the strict build); whether `spectio.pages.dev` serves this site at that path is **[to be validated]** once the docs are deployed there.
2. **The list is any recent pipeline, named "test".** It holds every location the server ran, not only test pipelines; on a shared server it would have to be scoped to the person (`security-reviewer`, round 1 note). 0.0.1 runs one server per person ([dev-env §15.3](../../specs/dev-environment-and-testing.md#153-001-on-a-cluster-architecture-and-safety-without-accounts)).

**Process:**

- Follow-up FU-P3-17: API input hardening, before the API is served beyond local mode (P1 Task 18 at the latest, which adds the read endpoints). It covers:
    - every refusal is a 422 or a 403, never a 500: `ENAMETOOLONG` on a location, a malformed `Referer`, a self-referential or malformed schema, a read error after the open (and the descriptor closed when `fstat` fails);
    - caps on the request body, the location, the params and their depth; a time bound on schema `pattern`s **[to be validated]** against `jsonschema`'s options;
    - uvicorn's access log off, or without the query string;
    - `refuse_control` also refuses Unicode format and separator characters (categories Cf, Zl, Zp);
    - the `NO_API` text no longer names the Host checks as missing.
- Follow-up FU-P3-18: Git fetch and runner hardening, before a Git location is launched on a login node (P1 Task 22 at the latest). It covers:
    - a size cap per clone and a way to remove old clones;
    - git run with a minimal environment (no `GIT_ALLOW_PROTOCOL`, `GIT_CONFIG_PARAMETERS`, `GIT_CONFIG_COUNT`), and no LFS smudge (`GIT_LFS_SKIP_SMUDGE=1` **[to be validated]** against git-lfs's docs);
    - a cache key with the scheme, the port and the ssh user; the ssh user validated, never starting with `-`;
    - a launch whose head can never be adopted again is reported ended, not retried on every tick.
- Follow-up FU-P3-19: API test hygiene, at the next change of the API tests (P1 Task 18 at the latest). It covers:
    - no `StarletteDeprecationWarning`: the dev dependency `httpx2` in place of `httpx`. Starlette 1.10.0, the locked version, imports `httpx2` first and warns only when it falls back to `httpx` (`starlette/testclient.py:26-41` in the worktree's `.venv`, read 2026-10-30); whether `httpx2` installs cleanly beside FastAPI 0.162.0 is **[to be validated]**;
    - `monkeypatch.setattr(wf, "run_git", fetch)` in `test_launch.py`;
    - `test_inspect_symlink_out_of_tree_not_read` asserts one status;
    - whether `test_sigterm_stops_cleanly` is flaky, and why.
- **Status after Task 18:**
    - FU-P3-14 is done (`df61c4e`).
    - FU-P3-17 is done (`4bddad3`, `562510b`) except the time bound on schema `pattern`s, which moves to FU-P3-20 with an owner (CR-P3T18-04 (r1), SEC-P3T18-06 (r1)).
    - FU-P3-19 is done except its last item, whether `test_sigterm_stops_cleanly` is flaky and why. Its 15 isolated passes do not cover the full-suite case of Task 17's round 1 (CR-P3T18-04 (r1)). It stays open, due before the M4 done proof.
- Follow-up FU-P3-20: stream and refusal hardening, before the API is served beyond local mode (P1 Task 16 at the latest; the log-owner item before P1 Task 25). It covers:
    - a size bound on a delta frame's `changed.tasks`, or a frame that says "too many, refetch" (SEC-P3T18-03 (r1));
    - the log reader checks that the file is owned by the user, as dev-env §15.3 req. 12 says, before P1 Task 25 reuses it for `cli-observed` launch directories (SEC-P3T18-05 (r1));
    - a time bound on schema `pattern`s, from FU-P3-17 **[to be validated]** against `jsonschema`'s options;
    - closed streams free their cap slot at once under ASGI spec 2.4 too, and `test_client_cap_holds_under_concurrent_opens` waits for the closes rather than sleeping 0.2 s (CR-P3T18-04 (r2));
    - the framework-refusal handler survives a status outside `http.HTTPStatus` (SEC-P3T18-05 (r2));
    - a `405` lists every method its path serves in `Allow` (the `allow: POST` of the Evidence transcript).
- Follow-up FU-P3-21: a typed OpenAPI document, before Task 19 generates its client from it. It covers:
    - response models for the routes, so the 200 schemas are not free objects, and an `info.version` that a release bump does not change (CR-P3T18-03 (r1));
    - the task cursor: keep `[id, id]` or move to a single value while 0.0.1 has no clients; either way the redundant clause goes (CR-P3T18-05 (r1));
    - the unused `monkeypatch` and the in-function import of `test_client_cap_holds_under_concurrent_opens` (CR-P3T18-05 (r2)).
- **Status after Task 19:** FU-P3-21 is not done. Task 19's client is hand-written for three routes (#88 rule 4), so FU-P3-21 moves on whole, owner P1 Task 20, the next client of the read API.
- **Status after Task 20:** FU-P3-21 is done except generating `web/src/api.ts` from the snapshot, which moves to P1 Task 21 (#90 rule 8): response models on every read route and `:cancel`, `info.version` pinned to `0.0.1` (CR-P3T18-03 r1), the one-ID task cursor (CR-P3T18-05 r1), and the `test_stream.py` tidy (CR-P3T18-05 r2), all in `d12a0e2`.
- Follow-up FU-P3-22: screen-serving hardening, before the screens are served beyond local mode. It covers:
    - `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` and a `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy` on every screen answer (SEC-P3T19-03), before P1 Task 20 adds run pages that may link out; the COOP value **[to be validated]** against what the run pages of Task 20 open. **Done in G4** (`5d1eed7`), with `same-origin`; the value is still to be checked against Task 20's run pages;
    - the screens directory read from the wheel's package data first, and otherwise checked to be owned by the user and not writable by group or others (SEC-P3T19-01), with P1 Task 21;
    - `npm ci --ignore-scripts` in the server's "not built" text and in the docs, or `ignore-scripts=true` in a `web/.npmrc`, so that local installs match CI (SEC-P3T19-02). Whether the Vite build still works without esbuild's install script on every platform is **[to be validated]**; it did in CI's `ubuntu-latest` and in this session;
    - `HEAD` on the screen routes and assets (the `405` of the Evidence transcript);
    - a script that makes the `app-launch` shot again: it starts `spectio serve` in a demo home, stages the pipeline and removes it afterwards (CR-P3T19-05);
    - one shared module for the three `O_NOFOLLOW` walks (`api/workflows._open`, which `static.py` now imports though it is private, and `api/logs._open`), a simplifier note.
- **Status after Task 20** (FU-P3-22): `npm ci --ignore-scripts` in the "not built" text (SEC-P3T19-02) and `HEAD` on the screens are done (`d12a0e2`); the COOP value is checked against Task 20's pages (open point 5 above). SEC-P3T19-01 moves to P1 Task 21 with the wheel. The shot script gains a teardown item: remove the demo account and `/g/demo-lab` after the capture (SEC-P3T20-05).
